## Full-read pass of modules 1–11 — findings to decide (11 Oct)

Seven full reads (AG-02, AG-04, AG-05, AG-06, AG-07, AG-08, AG-09, AG-10, AG-11, AG-13, AG-15, CP-01). Decided items are moved into the sections above as they are settled.

**Decisions needed (ranked by impact):**
1. ✅ **DECIDED 11 Oct — by sending route:** on ExpatReady's shared email, marketing is **not sent** to people with no recorded marketing consent ("180 people have no recorded marketing consent and won't receive this") — protects ExpatReady's shared sending reputation; from the firm's **own connected mailbox**, a warning only ("Send anyway?") — the firm decides; **WhatsApp:** *(corrected 11 Oct after checking Meta's policy)* **any** message the firm starts — service or marketing — needs the client's WhatsApp opt-in (Meta: "You may only contact people on WhatsApp if … you have received opt-in permission from the recipient", whatsappbusiness.com/policy); without opt-in the message goes by email; replies inside the 24-hour window need no template; opt-out / block requests must be honoured. Each template has a **purpose** (service / marketing): ExpatReady default, firm may change. All CASL / Law 25 / "within the law" claims removed. *Was:* **Marketing-consent block before sending** — AG-10-22, BR-2/3, AG-02 BR-9 / AG-2-33, AG-11 BR-8: ExpatReady refuses marketing sends without recorded consent and classifies each message transactional / marketing "under CASL". Rule 7 vs ExpatReady's own sending reputation; WhatsApp opt-in is a Meta rule.
2. ✅ **DECIDED 11 Oct — send in the firm's main language and tell the firm:** an automatic message goes on time in the firm's **default language**; the client record shows "Sent in English — no French version"; the templates screen shows a standing notice per template ("no French version — 4 French-speaking clients received it in English this month. Add French?"). Manual-send prompt uses the same rule ("…will be sent in [firm's main language]. Send anyway?"). *Was:* **Automatic sends with no French version** — rule 7's "Send anyway?" covers manual sends only; docs fall back silently to the firm default (AG-02 AG-2-31a, AG-04 BR-8, AG-15 BR-10, AG-11 :327). Also "English" vs "firm default".
3. ✅ **DECIDED 11 Oct. (a) REPLACED 12 Oct by SA05-R9a — nothing is held; see the end of this file.** (a) The one-time screen is **"Review your automatic messages"**; it **lists every automatic message** but **holds back only self-firing ones** (timer/rule: chases, follow-ups, going-cold, onboarding series, countdowns, overdue-invoice reminders, booking reminders) until the Owner confirms; **direct responses to someone's action** (booking confirmation, invoice email after "Send", signing request, security emails, document-received) send from day one with ExpatReady wording; dashboard banner "Your automatic reminders are paused until you review them". (b) **ExpatReady improving an unedited message:** Owner and Admins get **7 days' notice** (templates screen + one email, old vs new side by side) with **"Keep my current wording"** (freezes the old wording as a firm-edited message); otherwise the new wording starts after 7 days. Knock-ons: AG-02, AG-07, AG-10, AG-11, AG-12. *Was:* **Option C details** — screen name/scope ("standard" vs "automatic" messages; AG-11 screen covers recipe messages only); unedited messages change when ExpatReady improves them — firm has not approved the new wording (AG-11 :31, :309); AG-02 / AG-07 / AG-10 also need option C knock-ons.
4. ✅ **DECIDED 11 Oct — can't delete while in use:** any template used by an automation (ready-made or firm-built) cannot be deleted; "Used by 'Going-cold follow-up' (on). Switch that automation off, or choose a different message for it, then delete." *Was:* **Templates used by a recipe can be deleted** (library) → run fails to dead-letter (AG-11 :919, :1067) — conflicts with rule 4.
5. ✅ **DECIDED 11 Oct — one sender per message:** the booking module sends only the booking lifecycle (confirmed, reminders, updated, cancelled, payment); **Missed appointment** is sent only by the No-show recovery recipe when the meeting is marked **no-show**; **Thank you / next steps** only by the Post-consultation recipe when marked **attended**; AG-04's timed `AFTER_MEETING` rule is removed; both recipes stay **off by default**, offered on the review screen. *Was:* **Double senders** — Booking's automatic messages vs recipes (no-show, after-meeting); AG-04 `AFTER_MEETING` may thank a no-show; Welcome / portal invite / document request vs the onboarding recipe.
6. ✅ **DECIDED 11 Oct — case opened, one welcome email:** onboarding starts when **a case is opened** (not at retainer signing); it sends **one "Welcome — your case has started"** email with portal set-up (or "Log in to your portal" if already invited) and the first to-dos (questionnaire, documents) waiting in the portal; a **returning client's new case** gets a short "Your new case has started" version (no portal set-up); **stream change / re-application** sends no automatic welcome. Portal invitation, questionnaire invitation and document request remain separate templates for manual and later sends. *Was:* **Onboarding trigger and set** — AG-07 :372/:392: case created (not retainer signed); welcome + portal invite + questionnaire link (map §9 says questionnaire + document request); "Add client" sends nothing (AG-7-74); welcome re-fires on a returning client's new case / stream change / re-application.
7. ✅ **DECIDED 11 Oct — one countdown for every dated government step:** AG-11's Biometrics countdown is widened into **"Government deadline countdown"** (on by default) for any step the pack marks "has a deadline" (biometrics, ADR, passport request, medical…); reminders count back from the **recorded due date** at default points **30 / 14 / 7 / 3 / 1 days** (firm-editable), skipping points before the request; the request message itself goes the day it is recorded. The **due date is required** when staff record a deadline step, **pre-filled** from the pack's usual timeline, editable for extensions (countdown recalculates). A data-entry rule, not a legal lock. *Was:* **Client deadline countdowns (ADR, government deadlines)** — mapped Must, but AG-11 has no Release 1 client countdown except biometrics and status expiry.
8. ✅ **DECIDED 11 Oct — closing letter dropped entirely** (no ExpatReady starter, no prompt; firm's own practice). *Was:* **Closing letter (Must) has no trigger** in AG-07 (cases close themselves).
9. ✅ **SETTLED 11 Oct (follows the earlier decision):** no ExpatReady cover / submission letter; AG-09 :298 reworded to "documents from the firm's own templates"; firms may create their own. *Was:* **Cover letters** — AG-09 :298 Drafter generates "cover letters" vs map §7 "no representative submission letter".
10. ✅ **SETTLED 11 Oct (standard design):** automation steps and booking reminder rules **point to a template**; no wording stored inside steps (needed for decisions 3 and 4). *Was:* **Template stored inside steps vs referenced from the library** — AG-02 `SequenceStep.templateVariantsByLocale`, AG-04 `ReminderRule.templateVariantsByLocale`.
11. ✅ **DECIDED 11 Oct:** **client documents** — firm-set keep-period (default none), deletion inside it = warning + reason, no pack retention numbers (as decision 6); **activity / audit log** — ExpatReady's own fixed security minimum (value to confirm with engineering / security; e.g. 2 years), the firm may keep longer, never shorter; product wording "kept for N years for security", never "legal obligation". *Was:* **Other retention locks** — AG-09 document retention from the pack ("as long as the law requires", PIPEDA); AG-13 audit retention "can't be set below its legal obligation" — extend decision 6?
12. ✅ **DECIDED 11 Oct — off by default, Owner can change:** financial and licence document types start not shareable (ExpatReady privacy default); the firm's Owner may mark any type shareable in firm settings, with a warning; copies are not shown on the worker's portal unless staff share them. *Was:* **Employer financial documents can never be shared** (AG-9-36, BR-17) — reason not stated.
13. ✅ **DECIDED 11 Oct — keep licensed-only** (consistent with the retainer licence block; data accuracy of the licence number on government forms): a law-society number counts as a licence; "Lead RCIC" / `LEAD_RCIC` label renamed "Lead representative"; a case without one still only warns at submission. *Was:* **Lead representative must be licensed** (AG-07 :224, :656) — kept exception like the retainer block, or a warning?
14. ✅ **DECIDED 11 Oct — import consent with a firm declaration:** when a consent column is mapped, the importer must tick "I confirm these people agreed to receive marketing from [firm], and I can show how and when"; consent is recorded as "imported — declared by <user>, <date> (source column …)" and counts as marketing consent; unsubscribe / STOP and bounce / spam suppression still apply. Replaces AG18-D10(i). *Was:* **Imported consent never written "until legal decides"** (AG-2-59, BR-19).
15. ✅ **SETTLED 11 Oct (applies rule 7 and the checklist decision):** to-do list starters are firm-owned, normal publishing, no SME gate; pack action lines are "ExpatReady's standard steps", hideable firm-wide; closing letter removed from the after-decision list. *Was:* **To-do templates tied to the pack + SME** (AG-13 :210, :483, :714) vs map §10 firm-owned starters; pack action lines hideable.
16. ✅ **DECIDED / SETTLED 11 Oct:** "Document accepted" = in-portal notice only, no email (AG-15 / CP-01 wording aligned); "Portal access paused" email stays Good to have (AG-15 knock-on); employer invitation merged into the portal invitation; invitation reminder Must for firms with the auto-nudge extra. **Portal timeline:** before submission the platform delivery steps (SA-1B-46; firm may rename — SA-1-38 / AG17-D32); after submission the pack's **government steps** (SA-1B-45 / 69) with due dates. Each government step carries a **staff name and a plain client name** written by ExpatReady per language (e.g. "Biometrics instruction letter (BIL)" → "Give your fingerprints and photo"); the firm may rename the client name in its copy; rules unchanged; no expert sign-off. *(Knock-ons: SA-01B government-step item gains a client label; AG-15-15; CP-1-23. Clean-up: SA-1-8 "disclaimer / compliance text" in the regulator setup becomes optional editable starter wording.)* *Was:* **Portal** — "document accepted" email or portal-only (AG-15 :275, CP-01 :252); "access paused" email (none in AG-15); case status labels as firm-editable client text (AG-15-15); employer invitation in R1.
17. ✅ **SETTLED 11 Oct (applies rule 4 and the earlier default):** the final notice (+7) is in the default sequence → **Must**; AG-06 aligned to the agreed defaults −3 / 0 / +3 / +7 (US-5 and AG-6-12 corrected). *Was:* **Dunning** — final notice (+7) is in the default sequence → Must (rule 4); AG-06 contradicts itself on the −3 days pre-due reminder (:215 vs US-5 / AG-6-12).

**Missing templates — decided 11 Oct:**
- **WhatsApp conversation opener** — **Must** for firms with WhatsApp connected; ExpatReady starter, firm-editable (re-approval by Meta on edit); used to reopen a conversation outside the 24-hour window (AG-10 :265, BR-31).
- **Refusal next steps** — **no separate template**; "Decision: refused" starter carries a neutral editable line ("We will contact you shortly to discuss your options"); explaining reconsideration / appeal / re-apply is the firm's advice (rule 7); AG-07's staff prompt stays.
- **Paid consultation cancelled** — **payment section in "Booking cancelled"**, shown only for paid bookings (refund full / partial / none + amount; firm's wording from a starter); arrives with the paid-cancel feature.
- **Returning employer** — "Employer questionnaire invitation" gets a **"please check and update" version**, used whenever the organisation's profile holds **any** saved answers (partial or old); the form shows filled vs blank and each answer's "last confirmed" date; no age limit. A returning employer = an organisation record in this firm with saved questionnaire answers (AG-8-39); never across firms; a duplicate organisation record is not "returning".
- **Pre-consult agreement** — own row in §2: Good to have (Must for firms with paid consultations); firm writes all of it from a starter (decided earlier).
- **Release 2 / later** (rule 5): credit note, refund receipt, SMS versions, call-recording notice.
- **Client consent screen (decided 11 Oct — on from day one):** one consent text per firm, shown at **portal activation** from day one with ExpatReady's starter wording; the firm may edit it or switch it off in firm settings; the questionnaire does not ask again once accepted; acceptance recorded with date, time and text version; a changed text is shown again at the client's next login; no expert sign-off. Listed in the new-firm setup checklist so the firm sees what clients agree to. *(Knock-ons: CP-1-13, AG-15-8, AG-08 :648.)*
- **Corrected decision / stream change / re-application (decided 11 Oct):** no automatic message and no new template; staff tell the client if needed with "Case update (general)"; the portal timeline shows the corrected facts or the new case.

**Missing templates found (pass list):** WhatsApp general opener for staff outside the 24-hour window (Must, AG-10 :265); refusal next-steps (AG-07 :524); pre-consult agreement row (AG-04 :214); paid-consult refund wording in "Booking cancelled" (AG-04 :361); credit note + refund receipt (Release 2, AG-06 :743); SMS versions (Release 2); call-recording notice (Release 2+); employer "confirm your stored details" (AG-08 :411); portal consent screen text (CP-1-13); corrected decision / stream-changed client messages (unclear).

**Map corrections (no decision needed):** §1 booking-born lead — knock-on to AG-02 and AG-04 (both still send the acknowledgement); §8 row "unsubscribe… (CASL)" → point to §17 #4, drop CASL; §8 Meta resubmission flow is still open (AG-10 Q18); §5 moments say "IRCC" → "<authority>"; §7 "Document received" belongs in §7 too; §9 onboarding row adds the portal invitation; §5 "Client / case opened" → "Case opened".

**Wording clean-up (rule 7, mechanical — applied when each module is written):** statute and regulator citations, "compliant / non-compliant", "immigration-validated", "within the law", mandatory disclaimers, SME routing of wording — AG-02, AG-04, AG-05 (full line list from the pass), AG-06, AG-07, AG-08, AG-09, AG-10, AG-11, AG-13, AG-15, CP-01.

**Design inputs for the shared template system (from the pass):** version recorded on every send; reach count before publishing; merge fields only from records (free-text body slot needed for "Reminder from your consultant" / "Request outstanding items"); missing field → approval, never a raw placeholder; no passport / financial numbers; AI-edited body → approval; per-template purpose (service / marketing); WhatsApp variant + approval status; quiet hours, rate caps, "Not started" skip; per-family-member and per-filing sends; conditional sections (video / phone / in-person); frozen issued documents (invoices, signed agreements); which template version a mid-run chase uses after an edit; to-do templates (anchor events, relative due days).

## Decisions after the SA-05 v3.0 / AG-12 v2.0 rewrite (11 Oct)

- **Q1 — Who confirms "Review your automatic messages":** anyone holding the template-publish permission (Owner and Admin by default); recorded (who, when); the Owner gets a one-line notice ("Client M confirmed your automatic messages"). Supersedes the map's "until the Owner confirms"; matches AG-16 Q11.
- **Q2 — Client messages for case events staff record** (Application submitted, Biometrics required, Medical exam required, Additional documents needed, Passport request, Decision: approved / refused, Application update, LMIA employer equivalents): **offered at the moment of recording, ticked by default** ("☑ Also send 'Decision: refused' to Client A [Preview] [Edit]"); staff can untick, preview or edit; a direct response to a staff action, so **sends from day one** (not held by the review screen); the firm may set any of these to start **unticked**. Not a separate "Staff-rec" held class (SA-05 v3.0 Q4 superseded).
- **Q3 — Government deadline countdown at a new firm:** **not held** — works from day one in ExpatReady's wording (deadline safety); listed on the review screen so the firm can edit or switch it off. All other self-firing messages stay held.
- **Q4 — Held messages on confirm:** before confirming, a preview ("Confirming will send 4 messages now and schedule 18 more"); on confirm, **one catch-up per sequence** — the most recent missed message in each sequence is sent once; older missed points are skipped; every sequence then continues from today on its normal schedule. Held messages are not queued as "Needs you" approval items.
- **Q5 — Lead acknowledgement at a new firm:** **not held** — a direct response to the client's enquiry; sends from day one; listed on the review screen (edit / switch off). Booking-born leads still get no acknowledgement.
- **Q6 — Welcome email:** tied to **case opened** only (creating a client sends nothing unless "Save and invite to portal", AG-7-74). When Client H opens a case, a box appears: "☑ Send 'Welcome — your case has started' to Client A [Preview] [Edit]" — **ticked by default** (email goes on save); unticked = nothing sent, and staff send it later with "Send welcome" on the case; Edit changes this one email only; the firm can set the box to start unticked for everyone. A direct response to a staff action, so it sends from day one (not held). Returning client's new case → short "Your new case has started" version. Same box pattern as Q2. Replaces the AG-11 onboarding *recipe* firing on its own.
- **Q7 — Plans:** the **Templates area** (automatic messages, library, documents) is on **every plan** (foundation, not a feature); the **ready-made recipes** are on **every plan**, switched on/off by the firm; SA-03 #20 **Automations (sellable)** = only the firm **designing its own** automations and reminder chains; **dunning** (−3 / 0 / +3 / +7) is part of Biller on every plan (payment plans may still be sold separately); when an **extra is switched off** (WhatsApp, AI, portal auto-nudge…), its templates stay visible read-only, dependent sends stop or fall back (WhatsApp → email), nothing deleted. *(Knock-ons: SA-03 catalogue rows #2/#17/#20 notes; AG-06 :667; AG-11 recipes vs Automations.)*
- **Q8 — Switching off automatic messages:** **any** automatic message may be switched off **except Signing request and Portal invitation / set-your-password** (edit only — they deliver the feature); switching off a **Must** shows a warning ("Final notice is part of your overdue-invoice reminders… Switch off anyway?"); recorded (who, when). Client security emails are ExpatReady-owned and never switchable.
- **Q9 — Marketing edge cases:** (1) **automatic** marketing sends skip people without recorded consent on **every** route (no human to answer "Send anyway?"); logged ("15 skipped — no recorded consent"); (2) people who **unsubscribed or replied STOP** are **always blocked on every route** — ExpatReady's fixed function, firms cannot override; (3) the **route actually used** decides (fallback from own mailbox to shared email applies the shared rule) and the firm is told who was not sent.
- **Q10 — Default purposes:** Follow-up, Invitation to book a consultation, Call follow-up / next steps, Missed appointment, Thank you / next steps after consultation, Going-cold nudge = **service**; Nurture check-in = **marketing**. The firm may change any.
- **Q11 — Changing purpose / bulk:** anyone who can edit templates (Owner and Admin by default) may change a template's purpose; **marketing → service** shows a warning ("Service messages go to everyone, including people who haven't agreed to marketing. Use 'service' only for messages about the person's own enquiry or case.") and is recorded; **bulk campaigns** (SA-03 #28) are **always treated as marketing**, whatever the template says. Bounce / spam suppression and unsubscribe stay.
- **Q12 — WhatsApp approval (closes AG-10 Q18):** when a firm connects WhatsApp, **ExpatReady automatically submits** all starter WhatsApp versions to the firm's account; a firm **edit** is resubmitted on publish; approval status lives **per firm per template** (Approved / Waiting for Meta / Rejected with Meta's reason + "Edit and resubmit"); while waiting or rejected the message goes **by email**; on an ExpatReady update to an unedited template the new WhatsApp version is submitted at the **start** of the 7-day notice and the **old approved text keeps being used** until the new one is approved; WhatsApp versions exist for **short client messages** (automatic + library), not documents or long letters; the **opener** also needs the client's opt-in (Meta).
- **Q13 — Who changes the firm's legal-type settings** (retainer clause locks, e-sign consent text, client consent screen text / switch-off, keep-periods, shareable document types): **Owner by default, delegable** — one new permission "Manage legal settings" held only by the Owner role at the start; the Owner may grant it to a custom role (e.g. to an office manager); every change recorded. *(Changed by the product owner 11 Oct from "Owner and Admin".)* Everyday template actions (edit, publish, Keep my current wording, reset, switch off, confirm review) stay Owner and Admin. *(Knock-on: SA-06 new key, Owner-only seed.)*
- **Q14 — Urgent correction:** only ExpatReady's **Platform Owner** may mark a publish as an **urgent correction**, with a written reason, and only to fix errors (broken link, wrong fill-in field, factual mistake, meaning-changing typo — not tone or new content); it takes effect **immediately** for firms using the template unedited; those firms are told straight after (old vs new, "Go back to my previous wording" for 7 days); firms that edited the template get the usual "ExpatReady updated this" note; every urgent correction recorded with its reason. *(Knock-on: SA-06 `system/template:*` — urgent flag Platform-Owner-only; SA-07 Appendix A row.)*
- **Q15 — Retiring a starter firms still use:** firms using it **unedited** get it as **their own firm version**, frozen at the last wording, still working (automations included) but no longer following ExpatReady; firms that edited it keep their version; all are told ("ExpatReady no longer provides '…'. Your copy is kept as your own template."); firms that never used it just stop seeing it. No separate platform-wide "emergency stop" of a firm's message — an urgent correction (Q14) covers errors.
- **Q16 — Second approver:** none. ExpatReady's **Content author publishes** starter changes (legal-type included); the **7-day notice** is the safety net; the **Platform Owner** approves only legal sentences in ExpatReady's own emails (and urgent corrections, Q14). ExpatReady's **default e-sign consent text and certificate wording** also publish normally (no Owner sign-off, no SME). Content approver holds no template permission.
- **Q17 — Hiding standard items:** (1) **Owner and Admin** hide/show (`agent/case:configure-pack`), not `agent/pack:extend`; (2) **government steps (with deadlines) cannot be hidden** — the firm may switch off the countdown message or mark a step "not applicable" on one case; (3) hiding / showing applies to **newly opened cases only**; open cases keep the item and staff may mark it "not applicable" on that case.
- **Q18 — Smart Questionnaire (in conjunction with SA-01B):** follow **SA1B-D25 / SA-1B-93 / SA-1B-94 as decided** — firms add their own questions (firm-owned fields, never feeding scoring, forms or conditions); may **hide or reorder only questions ExpatReady marked optional** (SA-1B-7 placement requiredness); never change or remove required questions. SA-01B's "in their own copy" = a **firm layer over the live standard questionnaire** (added questions, hidden optional ones, order) — **no clones**; ExpatReady updates flow automatically; each case stays on the pack version it started with. **AG-08's clone-and-customise (AG-8-9 – AG-8-11) is withdrawn** and aligned to SA-01B. **New, Release 1: "who answers" switch** — for any standard question (required included) the firm may choose **client or staff**; a staff-answered question disappears from the client's form and staff fill it on the case; the data is still captured. The checklist "hide" rule (rule 7) applies to document checklists and to-do steps, not to questionnaire questions. *(Knock-ons: AG-08, SA-01B SA-1B-93 wording "own copy" → layer, plus the who-answers switch.)*
- **Q19 — Firm keep-periods:** **one firm setting** "Keep closed client files for ___ years" (default not set), covering everything in a closed case (signed agreements, documents, questionnaire answers, messages, LMIA candidate data); deleting inside it = warning + reason (recorded). **Release 1:** the setting, the warning and a list "N closed files are past your keep-period" — **no automatic deletion**; automatic disposal is a later release. The LMIA "record-keeping end date" (AG-17) is **calculated from the firm's setting** ("No keep-period set" otherwise). Activity log stays separate (ExpatReady security minimum, decision 11). Replaces the per-item keep-periods of decisions 6 and 11 with one setting.
- **Q20 — Consent loose ends:** (1) if the client has not accepted, the **consent screen appears before the questionnaire opens** (same one-time screen, wherever they enter first); (2) portal consent is **not** marketing consent — marketing needs its own tick; (3) when a firm switches the screen on later, clients who activated without it see it at their **next login or next questionnaire**; nothing blocked meanwhile; (4) the import declaration covers **email marketing** only — **WhatsApp opt-in** needs its own column and declaration ("…agreed to receive WhatsApp messages from [firm]"); anyone with the import permission may declare; recorded.
- **Q21 — Data requests and incidents:** a client's personal-data request is the **firm's**: handled in the firm's own tools (personal-data package in ExpatReady's fixed format + the firm's editable covering email, tracked in a firm-side request list); SA-11's register tracks only requests made **to ExpatReady itself**. Security incident: ExpatReady notifies the **affected firms** (Owner and Admins) with an ExpatReady-owned **"Security incident notice"** (replaces map §16 "Data breach notice to affected people"); the firm decides whether and how to tell its clients, with a library starter **"Notice to clients about a data incident"** (Good to have, editable); ExpatReady notifies people directly only for its **own users** (firm staff accounts).
- **Q22 — Welcome edge cases:** (1) imported cases: the welcome is a choice in the "Start client automations" preview, **unticked by default** for cases with progress; (2) families: **one welcome to the principal applicant** (CP-1-94); (3) no email: box greyed — "No email address — add one to send the welcome"; (4) no separate questionnaire invitation at the same moment as the welcome (the welcome lists it; the invitation stays for later re-sends); (5) emailed portal set-up links valid **7 days**; re-send issues a new link (replaces CP-01 BR-4's 5 minutes for emailed links).
- **Q23 — Paid consultations (closes AG-04 Q7):** a **per-appointment-type setting** ("Charge $150 to book") for firms with a **connected payment account** — no new plan extra; unpaid booking holds the slot **30 minutes** by default (firm-editable); "Complete your payment" at **10 minutes**; "Payment expired — time released" when the hold ends; per-type tick "Client must accept our pre-consultation agreement" (firm's document template), accepted version recorded on the booking.
- **Q24 — Licences:** **any representative type in the pack** (SA-1-8: RCIC, Lawyer …) passes the two kept licence checks (retainer "No active licensed consultant" block; lead-representative picker) — a lawyer records "Lawyer, Law Society of Ontario, #12345" on their profile (AG-01); wording is neutral ("licence number", not "CICC number"). The **footer licence text** is a firm-written layout setting (firm decides what to show); documents tied to one person (retainer) use **that case's lead representative's** licence automatically. Closes the "law-society number counts" to-confirm note.
- **Scope triage (5 Oct):** a review file — nothing moved. Template-related proposals stay undecided; if a feature later moves to Release 2, its templates move with it (rule 5). The catalogue notes this dependency.

**Obvious defaults O1–O32 — approved by the product owner, 11 Oct:**
O1 running chase uses the current published version at each send; an approval item keeps the version it showed · O2 a second ExpatReady publish during a notice shows the latest and restarts the 7 days · O3 switched-off / hidden templates: screen notice only, no email · O4 reach count = 4-week average; ExpatReady staff see counts only, never firm names · O5 firm created during a notice starts on the new version · O6 a new self-firing automatic message added after review starts held; new direct responses start at once · O7 firms existing before v2.0 see the review screen once; nothing already sending is paused · O8 an ExpatReady rollback is a new version and triggers the 7-day notice · O9 "Use the new wording now" and "Dismiss" allowed, recorded · O10 no firm-made conditional sections · O11 free-text-slot templates are manual sends only · O12 firms see ExpatReady-owned emails read-only · O13 delete block covers **any** reference (automations, appointment types, booking reminder rules); an **off** automation does not block deletion · O14 French-main firm with no French version → English, client-record note · O15 ExpatReady's own emails fall back to English; SA4-D19 (French first) stays for billing **documents** only · O16 adopt the co-product owner's must-keep fields for the older billing emails; add a "yearly to monthly" notice as an ExpatReady email · O17 keys: withdraw `agent/template:adopt`, `:resync`, `:archive`; fold `agent/communication:manage-templates` into `agent/template:*`; keep `agent/retainer:configure-template` (narrowed) and `agent/task:template`; add "Manage legal settings" (Owner, delegable, Q13); add a Platform-Owner-only urgent-correction flag (Q14) · O18 one sender for upload reminders — the AG-11 chase only; the Minder never messages clients · O19 auto no-show counts as no-show; "attended" = completed; short delay before the recipe sends · O20 dunning −3 / 0 apply to every invoice with a due date; client statement is a manual download · O21 an Owner lock or edit on an unedited retainer starter creates the firm's own version; signed agreements frozen · O22 returning client = any earlier case for the same client at the firm · O23 LMIA three-way check = a notice, not CRITICAL · O24 "Owner contact changed" upgraded to Must · O25 ExpatReady support sessions cannot confirm the review, keep wording or switch messages on/off · O26 countdown points, 7-day notice, dunning and signing defaults fixed in code now, admin-adjustable later · O27 ExpatReady neutral branding when a firm has no layout · O28 assessment: shared copy frozen at the version used; no disclaimer on staff screens; full language fallback; edited under template permissions · O29 "Document received" once per upload session; infected-file message is fixed ExpatReady wording · O30 no separate pack "automation templates" — the pack supplies deadline data used by the Minder and the Government deadline countdown · O31 "AI-edited" mark on the template version; clears only on reset or a non-AI rewrite · O32 pack additions: a "standard step" (action line) kind, a "usual timeline" field, law-society licence formats.

## Parked for later (rule 8)

*(Filled as modules are reviewed. Pending the product owner's review — suggestions on the 11 Oct decisions, to be tagged with the SA-05 / AG-12 review: Q14 urgent-correction route → park; Q18 "who answers" switch → park; firm template version history/restore → simplify to one firm version + reset; reach count → park; missing-French monthly counts → simplify to a badge; "ExpatReady updated an item you've hidden" note → park; WhatsApp versions → simplify to opener + ~5 key messages; review-screen preview count → simplify (keep catch-up); "Manage legal settings" → keep.)*

## Complexity review (founder lens, rule 8) — decisions

- **R1 — Custom-role editor:** **kept as decided** (SA6-D3, SA3-D18 — firms build their own roles, every plan). Not parked. Q13 "Manage legal settings" stays Owner by default, delegable through a custom role.
- **R2 — How the role editor shows permissions:** the custom-role editor shows **~25–30 plain-language capabilities** (each a bundle of the underlying permissions, e.g. "Work on invoices and payments", "Give refunds", "Change billing settings"); an **"Advanced" toggle showing every individual permission is in Release 1**. All 259 permissions stay in code, checked and audited as specified; starter roles unchanged. *(Knock-on: SA-06 — define the capability groups and the mapping; AG-01 role screens.)*
- **R3 — Team safeguards:** **kept as specified** (AG11-D9, AG-16 §3.10, AG-11-80 – 84: four parts, auto-on at 5 staff, grace, banner, reminders, per-part settings, backup approver).
- **Review scope (the product owner, 11 Oct):** bring **everything**, including items that would reverse earlier decisions, one at a time.
- **R4 — One sign-in at a time for firm staff:** **kept** (SA7-D67 point 8). Recorded reason worth adding to SA-07: it also prevents seat sharing (several people on one paid seat).
- **R5 — Supervised client messages: removed entirely** (the product owner: "we don't need it at all… let firm decide… decisions like this is being a regulator"). Withdraws AG11-D11(c), AG-11-82, AG-10-49, AG-16-47 and related. **Also removed — licence-based approvals:** the licensed-approver requirement for AI-written client text (AG11-D11(a), AG-10-44, AG-7 BR-23), the "who may approve AI text" setting (AG-16-46), and the licensed approval for switching on an imported client (AG-18-86, AG18-D11(b)). **Kept:** AI-written client text is approved by a human (any staff with the permission) before sending — ExpatReady's own product safety, not regulation; and the two kept licence rules (retainer needs an active licensed person; lead representative licensed — data accuracy). Rule 7 extended: ExpatReady does not decide who must check whose work.
- **R6 — 7-day notice, simplified:** keep the 7-day notice with "Keep my current wording" (decision 3b) and the urgent-correction route (Q14). **Dropped:** restart of the 7 days on a second publish (O2 — a second publish updates the notice text; the switch date stays) and special handling of firms created mid-notice (O5 — a new firm simply uses the current published version).
- **R7 — AI-edited templates:** approval happens **once, at publishing** ("This wording was edited with AI. Publishing confirms you've reviewed it." counts as the human approval); sends then go normally. One-off AI-drafted messages still need approval each time. The "AI-edited" mark stays on the version for the record. Replaces SA-5-75's every-send rule and O31's clearing rule.
- **R8 — ExpatReady staff roles:** **four visible roles** — Owner (everything incl. staff, security, billing approvals, legal sentences), Operations (onboarding + billing + support), Content author, Content approver (second person for country packs, kept); the engineer keeps deploy access outside the console (SA8-D1). **Staff-role editor parked** (SA7-D2 / SA-7-27); roles stored as data, seeded by release; all `system/*` permissions stay in code. Replaces SA7-D8's nine roles.
- **R9 — Live plan / price changes:** an **Owner changes them directly**, behind a confirm screen listing affected firms plus a second-step sign-in check; every change audited (who, when, before/after); the **30-day notice to existing firms stays** (SA-4-48). Second-approver queue parked (SA3-D8b, SA-3-44, SA4-D5 approval part); request record kept as the audit record.
- **R10 — Taking something away from a live plan:** **never remove; retire and replace** (grandfathering — Stripe prices are immutable; industry practice). Live plans only gain features (immediately for all). To remove a feature or lower a limit: create a new plan and retire the old; existing firms keep what they bought until they change plan. **Price increases** keep the decided 30-day-notice rule (SA-4-48). Per-firm scheduled removal engine parked (SA3-D8, SA-3-43); per-firm plan-version field may stay in data. A later legacy migration, if ever needed, is a one-off project with notice.
- **R11 — ExpatReady analytics (SA-09):** **one "Business" page** — ~8 nightly numbers (firms by status, new / cancelled this month, trial conversion, monthly active users, a few module counts) vs last month, a **feature-usage table** (firms using each feature, last 30 days), plain audited CSV export. **Record feature-usage events from day one** (data foundation). Revenue from Stripe's reports (SA4-P1). **Parked:** revenue dashboards, FX / multi-currency, per-country views, alert-rules engine, metric catalogue, six role dashboards, health graphs (SA8-D12), aggregation pipeline beyond one nightly job. A stuck-queue / failed-job line goes on the SA-08 status page.
- **R12 — Support tool (SA-10):** **simple queue** — ticket form ("what's wrong" + screenshot; **support sets priority**, not the firm), one queue oldest-unanswered first, manual assignment, internal notes never visible to firms (incl. the security fixes for today's code), email on support reply, "open support session" link from a ticket, tickets return to queue when staff leave; show ticket age and "waiting on firm"; **record first-response time from day one**. **Parked:** SLA engine, escalation, routing / round-robin / auto-triage, canned replies, knowledge base (static help pages outside the product), satisfaction survey, metrics suite, settings screens. **Template knock-on:** "Satisfaction survey" email dropped from the SA-05 catalogue (rule 5).
- **R13 — Audit and data governance (SA-11):** **solid basics** — append-only audit log (actor type, firm, correlation id), database grant allows insert only, failed writes retried with an alarm, support-session logging, ExpatReady staff-action records kept 7 years, breach register (plain record); reserve a sequence-number field. **Parked:** hash chain and verify job, sealed off-site copies, two-person audit deletion (no deletion path in the product), investigation console (a filtered list + audited CSV instead), compliance dashboard. **Clean-up confirmed:** retention editor / floors / archive / auto-disposal → Q19; residency generator → written statement (SA8-D9); firm-client data requests and client breach notices → firm (Q21); legal-hold register parked (nothing auto-deletes in Release 1).
- **R14 — Feature flags / platform settings (SA-12):** **keep** flags with two scopes (platform on/off + per-firm override), server-side flag-AND-plan check, "why is this on for this firm", kill switch with a reason, platform-wide maintenance mode with a message, one platform banner (EN/FR, start/end), all changes audited, "no flag can switch off security", client-records read-only rule; keep a scope-kind field in data. **Parked:** % rollouts / sticky hashing / allow-deny lists / rollouts dashboard, user and country scopes, settings registry beyond the security settings (rest = code / deploy config), per-firm rate and upload limits, per-firm maintenance + bypass tokens, per-firm banners. **Clean-up:** remove "firm override to close a deal / paid trial" text (contradicts SA-12-10, SA3-D6).
- **R15 — Firm-editable timings: kept as decided.** Editable: Government deadline countdown points (decision 7), document / questionnaire chase cadence, the five AG-16 §3.13 timings (AG11-D17), signing reminder days (§3), overdue-invoice reminder days (§4), booking reminder timing per appointment type (§2), paid-booking hold (Q23). **O26 ("fixed in code now") is withdrawn** — it contradicted these decisions.
- **R16 — Appointment-type options (AG-04): kept as specified** (hosting models incl. group / collective, pool distribution choice, caps per day/week/month per host, refund tiers, per-type video provider, five categories). **Fix applied regardless:** each staff member manages their **own availability** and connects their **own calendar** (AG-04 :722, :732 currently Owner/Admin only).
- **R17 — Portal visibility and policy:** **what staff send to the client is visible in the portal** (requested documents, questionnaires, invoices, agreements); kept: portal on/off per case, messaging on/off, "Show firm-produced documents" (default off). **Parked:** the six per-case visibility switches (AG-15-14; fields stay, default on) and the firm **portal policy screen** (AG-15-41) — fixed defaults: invite links 7 days (Q22), client two-step sign-in off, languages = platform-supported, "stalled" after 7 days, weekends excluded. Fixes CP-1-30's triple gate.
- **R18 — Billing settings (AG-06 / AG-16):** **continuous invoice numbering** (firm sets prefix + next number; never resets; year may be put in the prefix); **tax:** firm picks its province, standard rate from the pack (ExpatReady keeps rates current — data accuracy) + **per client or invoice "Taxable / Zero-rated / Exempt", chosen by the firm** (rule 7); no tax-profile editor; **currency:** multi-currency supported, **one currency per case** (all invoices, payments, receipts in that case use it); **no FX feed, no conversion** — reports and totals shown per currency, never summed; reporting-currency and FX-source settings removed; **payment plans:** an invoice per instalment only; payment-plan templates parked; **payment terms:** one firm default "Due in N days" + per-invoice due date (defines `agent/billing-config:terms`); **add offline payment instructions** (one text block on invoices and the "Invoice sent" email). Per-office numbering only if a second office exists; data fields (office, tax components, currency) stay.
- **R19 — Dashboards and sales reporting (AG-02, AG-14):** **one dashboard with sections** (Sales, Cases, Money, Workload, Deadlines), each shown by the viewer's permissions, figures "mine / everyone" by key; sales basics: funnel counts, leads by source / campaign, conversion rate, lost-by-reason, pipeline value **per currency**; scheduled reports weekly / monthly to self + named users, in-email summary + PDF. **Parked:** weighted forecast, commit / best case, quotas, rep leaderboard, win-probability per stage, load targets and flags, role-based report recipients, six role dashboards, `report:configure`, `report:view-compliance`. Data kept: expected close date, stage history.
- **R20 — Staff notification settings:** **personal settings only** — each person: email on/off for ~5 groups (Leads, Cases & deadlines, Clients & messages, Billing, Firm & security), one digest choice, notification language; in-app bell always on; push waits for a mobile app; urgent events (deadlines, security, failed payments) always sent. **Parked:** firm notification matrix, firm quiet hours, firm digest (AG-16-9, AG-16-33, AG-10-29b; policy table may stay in data). Market reference: MyCase per-user notifications; firm-wide matrix is a large-firm feature (Clio Operate).
- **R21 — Subscription discounts: kept as decided** (SA4-D1, SA4-D30; SA-4-42/43, SA-4-83 – 87).
- **R22.1–R22.5 — kept as decided:** "Start my subscription now" (SA4-D29d), staff yearly→monthly credit tool (SA4-D26), combined downgrade + switch-to-yearly rule (SA-3-60 / SA-4-77), in-console Stripe message log with re-run (SA-4-36), account-manager handover prompt (SA7-D64/65, SA-2-26).
- **R22.6 — Support "make changes" window:** **fixed 7 days**, revocable any time (replaces the 1 / 7 / 30-day choice, SA-2-28).
- **R22.7 — Firm web address:** **parked** — fixed after sign-up; support / engineer changes it on request (SA-2-27 parked; keep the redirect record shape in data).
- **R22.8 — Super Admin firm list and sign-up link tracking: kept as decided** (SA-2-3 nine filters; sign-up link 'opened' tracking).
- **R23 — Lead routing rules (AG-02): withdrawn — keep as specified.** The routing screen is already built in the frontend (`ERv2-frontend/src/features/pipeline/prospect-settings/components/prospect-assignment-rule-pane.tsx`, rule editor with source and programme conditions; runs on mock data; no backend yet in `ERv2-sanitized` @ db646bf7).
- **Rule added (the product owner, 12 Oct):** before recommending to park or simplify anything, check the code (`ERv2-sanitized` backend, `ERv2-frontend` frontend). **If it is already built, it stays.**
- **Process (the product owner, 12 Oct):** SA documents are final from the product owner's side — **SA cuts are decided now**. AG documents have not yet been reviewed by the product owner — **AG complexity items stay in the register** (scratchpad `complexity/` reports) and are brought **module by module during his AG review**, each with what the AG says, its build state in code, and a recommendation. R24 (firm-added questionnaire questions — not built) deferred to the AG-08 review.
- **Approval routing (defect, 12 Oct):** **SA-06's rule wins** (SA6-D9) — professional approvals go to every holder of the approval permission; the Owner approves when nobody holds it; no licence-based routing (follows R5). AG-16 BR-22 corrected during the AG updates.
- **Register saved (12 Oct):** `FINAL-SuperAdmin-FR/COMPLEXITY-REGISTER_2026-10-12.md` — the lens, the code-build check and the nine reviews.
- **Code-check outcomes (12 Oct, rule "built stays"):** **reversed to kept** — R9 second-Owner approval of live plan / price changes (built: `plan-change.service.ts:412`, approval-queue screen real); R10 per-firm plan versions and removal at renewal (built: `renewal-repin.service.ts`, `entitlement-reduction-notice.service.ts`); R22.6 support window **1 / 7 / 30 days** (backend built `firm-support-access.ts:93-110`; firm-side grant screen not built); R22.7 web-address change with 90-day redirect (built as a **Super Admin action** only); R13 hash chain, verify job, WORM off-site anchoring and legal-hold API (built). Still parked: two-person audit deletion (not built).
- **R8 revised:** ExpatReady staff roles = **the 6 already in code** (owner, admin, support, billing-ops, content-publisher, auditor — `role-template.platform.ts`); staff-role editor stays parked (frontend mock only).
- **Mock-only screens** (frontend on sample data, no backend) — decided case by case:
  - **R11 analytics console:** keep the **Business** and **Usage** tabs (wire to a real backend, matching R11); remove countries, health, alert rules, metric catalogue, role views and support tabs.
  - **R13 compliance console:** wire the screens whose backend is built — **audit search + CSV**, **integrity status banner** (verify job), **legal-hold list** (place / release); remove panels for parked or reassigned engines (retention engine, client data requests, residency generator).
  - **R14 maintenance and banners:** **platform-wide only** — platform maintenance with a message, one platform banner (EN/FR, start / end); remove per-firm maintenance, bypass tokens and per-firm banners from the screens.
  - **R18 invoicing settings, R19 forecast / quota / leaderboard:** AG items — deferred to the product owner's AG review (process decision).
- **Defect — only-Owner protection:** backend does not prevent deactivating / suspending the only firm Owner (`deactivate-lifecycle.service.test.ts:189-200`; frontend has a mock rule only). Spec + code fix required.
- **R25 — WhatsApp versions of ExpatReady starters: kept as decided (Q12)** — every short client message (~40) gets a WhatsApp version, auto-submitted per firm with per-firm status (not built today).
- **R26 — Questionnaire "who answers" switch: kept as decided (Q18)**, Release 1 (not built today).
- **The 9 pending template suggestions ("Parked for later", 11 Oct) — status:** urgent-correction route kept (R6/Q14); "who answers" kept (R26); WhatsApp versions all ~40 kept (R25); "Manage legal settings" kept (Q13); review-screen preview count, firm version history / restore, reach count, missing-French monthly counts, "ExpatReady updated an item you've hidden" note → **firm-side (AG-12 / SA-01B firm overlay) — deferred to the AG review**. Note: ExpatReady's own system-email version history and rollback are already built (`system-mail-template.controller.ts`, `version-history-panel.tsx`) and stay.
- **D1 — Pack "startable" check (SA-01B, not built):** fixed — a programme is startable when sections exist for every person, every condition resolves, and **every form on the row has a current edition**; the box-map requirement (SA-1B-90 / SA1B-D24) and box-map warnings (SA-1B-63 flag, SA-1B-92 "form boxes filled automatically") return with autofill (SA1B-D28).
- **Built SA items — stay as specified (12 Oct code check):** country suspend / re-enable (SA-1-5); pinned-firms-per-pack-version counts (SA-1-62); staff "require password change" (SA-7-41); overdue list with send reminder / update-card link (SA-4-24); monthly written-off report; system email templates core (list, edit, publish, versions, rollback).
- **Spec follows code (the product owner, 12 Oct):** adopt newer pack rules with a **required reason** (built backend, SA-1-58); reference tables — **6 table types fixed in code**, columns editable per version (SA-1B-41 – 44 amended); step-up window **fixed 5 minutes** (config); staff auto-logout = **one editable policy** under `system/platform-config:manage` — **must stay off for firm users and clients** (SA7-D67; flag to developers); support session **fixed 60 minutes** (config, SA-2-18); supervised export for a closed locked firm = **built backend + engineer runbook**, no screen (SA-2-37).
- **R27 — French on ExpatReady's own system emails:** **English required, French optional** (O15 confirmed); the built publish check requiring fr-CA for every key (`system-mail-template-admin.service.ts:370-379`) is to be **removed** — code change for developers. Billing documents (terms, invoices) stay French-first (SA4-D19).
- **R28 — Super Admin editor for firms' starter roles: kept as decided (SA6-D12)** (not built; existing mock screens follow the superseded "push to firms" model and need redoing).
- **R29 — ExpatReady staff password rules: kept as specified (SA7-D24)** — 12+ characters, breached-list check, **plus character mix and last-5 reuse** (the latter two not built: backend + password-history table needed).
- **R30 — Recipients of ExpatReady billing emails: two sets** — (1) documents (invoices, receipts, refunds, credit notes) → billing email, or the Owner if none set; (2) every other billing notice (payment failed, hold, trial reminders, card expiring, price change, plan changes, discount ending, cancellation) → billing email + Owner + Admins. Replaces the four risk-based sets (SA4-D8, SA-4-53). Code today: one rule (primary contact + billing email, `firm-billing-contacts.ts:27-52`) — needs changing.
- **R31 — Missing invoice copy:** **immediate alert + automatic retry** (failed copy retried; if still failing, engineer alerted on the SA-08 status page) replaces the daily exception report (not built). 6-year retention in Canada unchanged (SA4-D18).
- **R32 — SA-08 settings fixed in code:** e-sign evidence always captures time, IP, device and consent (no "evidence options" setting); mailbox / WhatsApp providers = Gmail, Microsoft 365, IMAP + one WhatsApp provider, added by release ("Super Admin approves" = a one-time product decision; emergency off via the kill switch, R14); upload limits stay the built constants 25 MB documents / 10 MB images (`upload-validation.ts:44-60`), not a SA-12 setting.
- **R33 — Legal wording in ExpatReady's own emails:** **email-level "contains legal terms" flag** (e.g. cancellation confirmation, refund notice, terms-related emails); publishing a flagged email needs the **Owner** role; Content author drafts; "never the drafter" rule dropped (avoids deadlock with one Owner). Replaces sentence-level legal markers (SA-5-56). Built today: single `system/mail-template:publish` step — add the flag and the Owner check.
- **Developer note — must-keep fields:** decided must-keep fields in ExpatReady's emails (SA-05 Appendix B; O16) to be enforced using the existing `required` flag (`system-mail-template.ts:168`); today only the link argument is enforced.
- **R34 — Pack maintenance (SA-01B, not built):** overdue review = "next review" sortable column in the pack editor + **weekly email to the content team** listing items past their review date + warning at publish; no queue screen (SA-1B-40 amended; review-date fields SA-1B-39 stay). "Sections used by nothing" list (SA-1B-17) **parked**.
- **R35 — Plan ladder (SA-03, not built):** each plan has a **ladder position**; saving a plan runs a **consistency check** that refuses, with a clear message, when a higher plan lacks something a lower plan has ("Professional must include Client portal, because Starter does"). **Auto-propagation of additions and renumbering on retire parked** (SA3-D12, SA-3-53 – 56 amended). "Everything in X, plus…" display unchanged. Undecided-feature handling in plan versions is built and stays (`plan-authoring.rules.ts`).
- **R36 — Reasons on role changes (SA-06 BR-23 / SA-6-18; AG-1-66; not built):** audit always records who, when, before / after; the **reason is optional**, except **giving or removing the Owner role**, which requires one.
- **R37 — Subscription invoices visible to:** `agent/subscription-invoice:view` seeded on **Owner, Admin and Billing / Finance** starter roles (view and download subscription invoices and credit notes). Cancel, downgrade and billing details stay Owner-only (SA4-D23 amended for viewing only).

## SA-05 v3.2 open questions — decisions (12 Oct)

- **SA05-Q1a — Classes of unclassified messages:** accepted pattern — staff-recorded event → class 2 (offered, ticked); timer → class 3; someone's own action → class 1; staff-chosen → library. Applied: Government fee requested, Officer contact, Your job ads are live, Change to your LMIA, Employer welcome, Employer questionnaire invitation → class 2; LMIA expiring: unused positions → class 3; Your assessment is ready, Booking updated, Payment received, Document needs replacing → class 1; After-approval guidance, Government fees due → library.
- **SA05-Q1b — "Please sign your forms":** **can be switched off** like other automatic messages (with the Must warning); only Signing request and Portal invitation stay edit-only (Q8).
- **SA05-Q2 — Internal template permission names:** the nine `system/template:*` keys in SA-05 §10 adopted as final; SA-06 lists them.
- **SA05-Q3 — Client open items (refines Q21; revised same day by the product owner):**
  - **Purpose:** track anything to be acted on for a client **outside** case processing and government-step tracking, so nothing is missed: client requests, complaints, regulator enquiries, anything else. It is a tracker, not a reply mechanism.
  - **(b) No new list:** open items are **AG-13 tasks** on the client (or lead) with a **category**: Client request · Complaint · Regulator enquiry · Other. AG-13 already has category, assignee, due date and status (AG-13-1, AG-13:206). They show on the client screen (AG-7-84) and in My Tasks. Visibility follows the client (AG-7-90). **No Owner-only rule.**
  - **Copy of my file** and **Delete my data** are the same task plus the built engine action (`dsr.controller.ts`, `dsr-export.job.ts`), not a manual tick. Default due date is 30 days, matching the built clock.
  - **SA-05 templates (emails only for copy and deletion; none automatic):**
    - (a) "Here is a copy of your file": **Must** library starter, editable.
    - (c) "Your request is complete": Good to have, staff-chosen, used for copy and deletion only. For a deletion it fills from the built outcome report (`governance.ts:144`).
    - Complaints, regulator enquiries and other items get no email from this.
  - **Knock-ons, for the AG review:**
    - AG-13 / AG-07: the categories and the engine actions on the client screen.
    - AG-02/04: tasks on leads.
    - CP-01: whether clients can submit a request from the portal (later).
    - The earlier SA-06 visibility-key row is dropped.
- **SA05-Q4 — Keep-period of message records (refines Q19):** one firm setting "Keep closed client files for ___ years" covers (1) send records, which follow the message inside a closed case; and (2) lead-only records (a lost lead's messages and shared assessment), counted from the day the lead is marked lost or closed. These appear in the same "past your keep-period" list, with no automatic deletion in Release 1 and no separate lead setting (rule 9). (3) The activity log is ExpatReady's security minimum of **7 years** (decision 11, SA-11-14), which the firm cannot shorten. Code: these records use the built `standard` keep category (`data-classification.ts:46-52`). AG knock-on (AG review): AG-02/04 lead closure date feeds the list; AG-16 setting text names leads.
- **SA05-Q5 — Adding a must-keep field later:** the Content author requests it and a developer adds it in a release. No admin-screen toggle. This matches the code: the variable list and `required` flag are code-owned and read-only to editors (`system-mail-template.ts:156-169`).
- **SA05-Q6 — Urgent correction during a 7-day notice:** **no special rule** (the product owner: edge case, keep it simple). The urgent correction fixes the wording in use now (Q14). Any pending improvement keeps its date; if it carries the same error, the Content author fixes it as an ordinary second publish (R6: the switch date stays). Firms that pressed "Keep my current wording" own an edited copy and get the usual "ExpatReady updated this" note (Q14).
- **SA05-Q7 — Retiring an automatic message:** an automatic message is never retired while its triggering event exists in the product. Otherwise Q15 applies as for starters.
- **SA05-Q8 — Consent texts:** ExpatReady's default e-sign consent text and portal consent-screen starter follow the same 7-day notice with "Keep my current wording" (one rule for all ExpatReady templates).
- **SA05-Q9 — Notice channel:** urgent-correction and retirement notices use the same channel as the 7-day notice: a templates-screen note plus one ExpatReady email to the Owner and Admins.
- **SA05-Q10 — Unsubscribe and marketing edges:**
  - Unsubscribe and STOP are **per firm**.
  - Hard bounces and spam complaints are **platform-wide**, because they protect the shared sending service (ExpatReady's own duty).
  - A hand-written one-to-one email is service; a send to a list is marketing.
- **SA05-Q11 — WhatsApp "Paused":**
  - Treated like Rejected: the message goes by email, and Meta's reason is shown.
  - No automated in-window acknowledgement in Release 1.
- **SA05-Q12 — Template keys by ExpatReady staff role:**
  - Platform Owner = `owner`.
  - Content author = `content-publisher`.
  - The other four roles (admin, support, billing-ops, auditor) hold no template key.
  - The second approver for country packs belongs to the staff-roles question set (SA-07), which is open.
- **SA05-Q13 — "Contains legal terms" flag at launch:** cancellation confirmation, refund and credit note, price-change notice, payment-hold notice, deletion warnings, security incident notice. The Platform Owner sets and clears the flag.
- **SA05-Q14 — Notice length:** the 7 days is a **code constant**, not a setting.
- **SA05-R9a — Automatic messages run from day one (the product owner, 12 Oct; replaces decision 3a's hold; applies to every SA, AG and CP document):**
  - **Nothing is held for a review.** Every automatic message, including self-firing ones (booking, invoice, signing and document reminders; chases; lead follow-ups; onboarding series; countdowns), runs from day one with ExpatReady's neutral wording, unless it is off by default (e.g. Going-cold).
  - **"Review your automatic messages"** becomes an **optional page**: a list of every automatic message with on/off switches. There is no confirm step, no gate and no "paused until you review" banner.
  - **Guard 1 — missing firm details:** a message that needs a firm detail that is not filled in (e.g. payment instructions, office address) is **not sent**. The firm sees a short note naming the missing detail, e.g. "Invoice reminders are waiting for your payment instructions — add them".
  - **Guard 2 — one-time notice:** the dashboard shows once: "Your automatic messages are on. See or switch them off." It informs and does not block.
  - **Unchanged:**
    - imported clients stay quiet until switched on (AG-18);
    - the 7-day update notice;
    - the switch-off rules (Q8);
    - WhatsApp opt-in;
    - class 2 (offered, ticked by default, at the staff action);
    - class 1.
  - Class 3 now simply means "self-firing" and has no hold. Rule 9 flag F1 in SA-05 is closed.
  - **Why:** vertical-SaaS value is working on day one; market norm is reminders on by default; operational neutral wording; the import quiet mode covers the legacy-client risk.
- **SA05-R9b — Loose ends of R9a (the product owner, 12 Oct; simplest answers):**
  1. **On/off permission:** the existing `agent/template:configure` (Owner and Admin). No new key.
  2. **Guard 1:** each message needs the firm details it uses (e.g. payment instructions, office address). If one is blank, that message is not sent. Once the detail is added, new messages go out; skipped ones are **not** sent late. The note shows on the dashboard to the Owner and Admins.
  3. **Missing details:**
     - **Missing firm detail:** Guard 1 applies.
     - **Missing client detail** (e.g. the client's name): AG-10-30's existing staff-approval route applies, unchanged.
  4. **Guard 2:** the Owner and Admins see it until one of them clicks "Got it". Pre-launch, so all firms are new.
  5. **New automatic messages ExpatReady adds later** arrive switched on, with the normal 7-day notice.

## ExpatReady staff roles — decisions (12 Oct, session 2)

- **SR-Q1 — Second approver (the product owner, 12 Oct; "keep it simple, not ideal from day 1"):**
  - **ExpatReady side:** two people may approve — **Platform Owner and Admin** both hold `system/starter-role:manage`, approver must differ from the requester (SA-06 OQ18 recommendation accepted). Packs and plan/price changes stay as built (Owner / Content publisher for packs; second Owner for plan and price changes). The server refuses to deactivate or suspend a second-to-last Owner so two Owners always remain *(recommended with the question; not separately confirmed — see open point below)*.
  - **Firm side:** **no two-person approval.** Whoever holds the right — Owner or Admin by default — acts alone.
  - **Open point (asked, not decided):** does "no two-person approval for firms" also remove the built-for-spec **Team safeguards four-eyes on government forms** (AG11-D9, R3 "kept as specified")? Code check: not built (no firm-side four-eyes code found in `ERv2-sanitized` or `ERv2-frontend`; the only matches are the pack-authoring self-approval checks).
- **SR-Q1b — Four-eyes on government forms (the product owner, 12 Oct): removed.** Follows SR-Q1 (no two-person approval for firms). Any holder of `agent/case:form-approve` may approve a form, including one they prepared. **Kept:** the "Self-approved" record on the form history and audit (data foundation). Not built in code. Withdraws AG11-D9 part 1 (knock-on to AG-11, AG-07, AG-16, 99-Glossary "Four-eyes" — applied in the AG review). Team safeguards part 4 (routing to licensed users) was already withdrawn by R5. Parts 2–3 (escalation, at-risk marks): next question.
- **SR-Q1c — Team safeguards switch removed (the product owner, 12 Oct).** No single switch, no auto-on at 5 staff, no grace, banner, quarterly reminder or Owner switch-off reason. What remains is two independent firm settings, both **on by default for every firm**, placed under **Advanced**: (1) **Escalation** — people who can reassign cases are alerted 2 days before a standard deadline and on the day; when the file owner is the only such person, they get one alert, not two; (2) **At-risk marks** on cases nearing a missed deadline. Not built in code. Replaces AG11-D9 and the defaults of AG17-D70 / AG17-D81 (knock-ons to AG-11, AG-16, AG-17, AG-07, 99-Glossary — applied in the AG review).
- **SR-Q2 — Who publishes ExpatReady's own emails (the product owner, 12 Oct): follow the code.** The **Platform Owner publishes every ExpatReady email**; Content publisher drafts and previews only (as built: `role-template.platform.ts:297-300`; earlier ruling O3, `DEFERRALS-REGISTER.md:371` `SA05-SINGLE-PUBLISHER`). The **"contains legal terms" flag and its Owner check are parked** (R33 and the SA05-Q13 flag list parked, not deleted). Firm starter templates (`system/template:*`) unchanged — Content publisher publishes (Q16). **Parked for later — two-lane publishing** (global norm): Content publisher publishes routine emails; emails flagged "contains legal terms" need the Owner. Trigger: content team grows or the Owner becomes a bottleneck. Cost later: one key on Content publisher + one yes/no field + one check — no data risk. Knock-ons (SA update, on approval): SA-05 SA5-D24, SA5-D53, Q13 row, §10; SA-07 Appendix A, OQ-8 (closed — moot), SA7-D70.
- **SR-Q3 — Staff role display names (the product owner, 12 Oct; closes SA-07 OQ-1):** Platform Owner · Admin · Support · **Billing** · **Content publisher** · Auditor. Code keys unchanged. "Content author" (SA-05, SA-06, this map) and "Billing operations" / "Security & audit" are replaced by these names when documents are updated.
- **SR-Q4 — Code vs SA-07 Appendix A reconciled (the product owner, 12 Oct; closes SA-07 OQ-2, OQ-3, OQ-5, OQ-6, OQ-10):** rule — code narrower for safety → keep code; role cannot do its basic job without a read → table wins. (1) Close a firm account: **Owner only** — remove `system/firm:close` from Admin (`role-template.platform.ts:203`). (2) Admin keeps `system/platform-user:audit-view` (table gains the tick). (3) Operational settings and feature-catalogue register: **Owner only**, as coded (table loses the Admin ticks). (4) Auditor gains read keys: staff list, Activity, firm list, billing status, support-session list. (5) Support gains read keys: firm list, health, payment-processor log, status page. (6) Billing gains read keys: firm list, entitlement view. (7) Content publisher keeps completeness profiles (`system/pack-policy:*`, `:287-289`) — table gains the tick. (8) Owner's `system/platform-user:suspend` / `:offboard` stay unused, no screen; spec stays "no Suspend, no Offboarded" (developer to confirm no route uses them). (9) Account manager = Admin (and Owner). **OQ-10:** the updated Appendix A is the target; remaining code differences are build tasks (seed lockstep `09-role-matrix-seed.sql`).
- **SR-Q5 — Pack approver does not edit (the product owner, 12 Oct; closes SA-07 OQ-4): follow the code.** The approver approves exactly what was submitted (frozen at submission, `pack-draft-review.service.ts:200-205`); to fix something the approver rejects with a reason, the author fixes and resubmits. Anyone who edits and resubmits becomes the submitter and cannot approve it. SA1-D6 per action unchanged; second person = Owner or another Content publisher (SR-Q1); test-only packs exempt.
- **SR-Q6 — Source of truth for staff roles (the product owner, 12 Oct; closes SA-07 OQ-7):** the **code registry is the master** (`role-template.ts` / `role-template.platform.ts`); the database is seeded from it by release; `check:seed-parity` (`apps/api/package.json:46`) blocks a release on any difference. Developer tasks: fix today's drift once; run the parity check in the release pipeline.
- **SR-Q7 — Reason on staff role changes (the product owner, 12 Oct; closes SA-07 OQ-9):** same rule as firm roles (R36) — reason **optional**, except giving or removing **Platform Owner**, which needs one; audit always records who, when, before / after; the reason is included in the Owner-change email to all Owners (SA7-D61). SA-07 OQ-8 is closed as moot by SR-Q2 (legal flag parked).
- **SR-Q8 — Starter-role editor view (the product owner, 12 Oct; closes SA-06 OQ19):** ExpatReady's starter-role editor uses the **same screen, the same 30 capabilities (SA-06 Appendix D) and the same Advanced view** as the firm custom-role editor — one component, built once.
- **SR-Q9 — Renaming a government step's client name (the product owner, 12 Oct; closes SA-06 OQ17):** `agent/case:configure-pack` (Owner and Admin by default; grantable via a custom role) — same key as hiding standard items (Q17).
- **SR-Q10 — Permission that approves AI-written client text (the product owner, 12 Oct; closes SA-06 OQ14):** **no new key** — approval uses the sending key (AG11-D2): `agent/communication:send-email` for email, `agent/communication:send-message` for WhatsApp / portal chat (AG-10 §10 :716-717). Anyone who may write to the client may approve AI text to that client.
- **SA-06 OQ15 (keep-period and delete-inside-it keys)** moved to item 2 (governance code), because it depends on `agent/retention-policy:manage` and the built retention controller.

## Governance code built against earlier "park" decisions — decisions (12 Oct, session 2)

- **G-Q1 — Disposal job (the product owner, 12 Oct): built stays, switched ON, with two fixes.** Code check: the job is not a keep-period deleter — it runs only on an **approved, window-elapsed "delete my data" request** (`disposal-executor.service.ts:46, 210-222`), refuses under an active legal hold, keeps classes with a floor (financial 7 years — `retention-policy.service.ts:30-60`). It is the engine behind SA05-Q3's "Delete my data"; switching it off would leave approved requests never executed. Q19 (no automatic keep-period deletion in Release 1) unaffected. **Fix 1 — grace from approval:** the recovery window today ends on the 30-day due date counted from receipt (`dsr.service.ts:314-319`, "no dedicated recovery-window config exists"), so a late approval leaves 0–1 days to undo; change to **7 days after approval** (code constant, like SA05-Q14); the request shows "Erasing on <date> — Cancel" during it. **Fix 2 — own switch:** the job boots with the general `WORKER_RUNTIME_BOOTSTRAP_ENABLED` (`worker/src/main.ts:489`); add a dedicated on/off switch for disposal only (emergency stop). Accepted consequence: an erasure approved after day 23 completes after the 30-day due date — the due date is a reminder, not enforced (rule 7).
- **G-Q2 — Consent records (the product owner, 12 Oct): built stays, ON, send-time check aligned to decisions.** Built: per-purpose append-only consent (`MARKETING_EMAIL`, `MARKETING_SMS`, `ANALYTICS_PROFILING`, `THIRD_PARTY_SHARING` — `contracts/schemas/governance.ts:28-48`), record / withdraw (`agent/consent:manage`), send-time gate (`rail-send-gate.service.ts`) that blocks only WITHDRAWN. **Align the gate (code change):** unsubscribed / STOP → blocked on every route (as today); no recorded consent → **skipped** on shared email and on any automatic marketing send; own mailbox, manual → "Send anyway?" warning (decision 1, Q9). **Add one purpose "WhatsApp opt-in"** (covers service and marketing; without it the message goes by email — Meta rule, Q12; fed by the import declaration, Q20). Analytics profiling and third-party sharing: kept in data, no screen (no feature uses them).
- **G-Q3 — Data-incident (breach) register (the product owner, 12 Oct: "leave it"):** no new work. **Firm side:** built backend (`breach.controller.ts`, `agent/breach:manage` on Owner and Admin) stays as is, **no firm screen** in Release 1. **ExpatReady side:** R13 unchanged (plain register kept; `system/breach:manage` route undelivered, Super Admin panel on sample data — delivery follows R13 as already decided). The recommended firm "Data incidents" page under Advanced was declined. Open for later: the decision step's regulator wording ("reasonable risk of significant harm", `rrosh-gate.service.ts`) stays in code only, never on a screen (rule 7).
- **G-Q4 — Keep-periods (the product owner, 12 Oct; closes SA-06 OQ15):** Q19's one setting stays a **reminder, not a lock** — drives the warning + reason and the "past your keep-period" list; it does **not** feed the disposal engine. Built per-class keep-periods (`retention-policy.controller.ts`, `retention-policy.service.ts:91-120`): **built stays, no screen**, dormant; ExpatReady's code floors (e.g. financial 7 years) unchanged. Keys: setting = `agent/legal-settings:manage` (Owner, delegable — Q13); deleting inside it = `agent/document:dispose` (Owner, delegable) with warning + reason; for a "delete my data" request the warning shows at approval and approving is the decision. `agent/retention-policy:manage` kept on the built route (Owner and Admin), no screen.
- **G-Q5 — Legal holds (the product owner, 12 Oct; closes SA-11 §13 Q13 and item 3 "legal-hold permission pool"):** **ExpatReady places holds — Platform Owner only**, from the Super Admin legal-hold list (R13 / SA11-D2). Add a platform route on the built service with key **`system/compliance:legal-hold`** (Platform Owner only). The firm route (`legal-hold.controller.ts`, `agent/legal-hold:manage`, Owner and Admin) **stays built, no firm screen** (dormant). Why: ExpatReady's day-97 purge of a cancelled firm refuses while any active hold exists (`firm-purge-sql.ts:39`, `firm-purge.service.ts:87`) — ExpatReady must be able to place holds (court orders) and firm-placed holds must not block the purge indefinitely. A firm user deleting held data sees "This data is on hold and can't be deleted." SA-11 §13 Q14 (governance modules under "built stays") is answered by G-Q1 – G-Q5.
- **G-Q6 — Client self-service data download (the product owner, 12 Oct): built stays, no portal button in Release 1.** Built: `portal/dsr-exports` request + portal download (`dsr-export.controller.ts:100-127`, `export-release.controller.ts:166`). Clients ask the firm; staff run the built export as decided (SA05-Q3); CP-01 decides the portal entry later. **Developer notes (mismatches, apply regardless):** (1) staff download is gated by `agent/pii:reveal` (`export-release.controller.ts:195`), a reveal key withdrawn by AG7-D10 — use `agent/data-export:request`; (2) export requests default to `exportMode = 'MASKED'` (`dsr.service.ts`) — AG7-D10 says exports are never masked; a client's copy must be complete.

- **SA-Q1 — Paid consultations and the payment-account extra (the product owner, 12 Oct; closes SA-03 §13 Q8):** (a) **Connecting the firm's own payment account is on every plan** — catalogue #26 keeps own email, calendar and WhatsApp only. Paid consultations (Q23, SA3-D25) and pay-by-card invoices (AG-6-14; "Invoice sent" pay-online link) work on every plan. Not built in code (only ExpatReady's own `stripe-billing.adapter.ts`). Possible later decision, not taken: a per-payment application fee via Stripe Connect. (b) **No payment-plan extra in Release 1** (R18: an invoice per instalment). Knock-ons: SA-03 row #26 and §13 Q8; AG-04, AG-06, AG-16 (AG review).

Changes apply to **new** signing requests only; staff cannot edit one agreement's expiry — an expired link is re-sent with **"Send again"** (new link, fresh full period). Not built in code. Knock-ons: SA-08 SA8-D21, SA-8-20, §3.8 list, entities; AG-05 AG-5-32 / AG-5-54 (AG review).
- **SA-Q3 — "Preferred" approver label and missing-credential note (the product owner, 12 Oct; closes SA-06 OQ13): both removed.** Follows R5 and rule 7. Not built in code. Approval items show every holder of the approval permission with no licence marking. The two kept licence rules (retainer "No active licensed consultant"; licensed lead representative) are unaffected. Knock-ons: SA-06 §3.3, SA-6-27, BR-20, AC5.2, AC5.4; AG-10, AG-11, AG-16 wording (AG review).
- **SR-Q1 correction (the product owner, 12 Oct):** the "refuse to deactivate the second-to-last Platform Owner" safeguard added under SR-Q1 is **dropped**. Floor stays **one** active Owner, as specified (SA7-D56, SA-7-25, BR-7) and as built (`platform-owner-guard.ts`, `errors.platform_user.last_owner` — counts active owners after the write, refuses zero). Reason: a compromised Owner account must always be deactivatable. With one Owner temporarily, an Admin is the second person for starter-role changes (both hold `system/starter-role:manage`). No code change.
- **R9-pass ruling 2 — Support "make changes" window (the product owner, 12 Oct):** built stays (`firm-support-access.ts:93-110`, 1 / 7 / 30 days). Firm screen: one toggle **"Allow support to make changes for 7 days"** (default 7, revocable any time); the 1- and 30-day choices under **Advanced**. **R22.6 ("fixed 7 days") is superseded** by the 12 Oct code-check entry.
- **R9-pass ruling 3 — Activity-log keep period (the product owner, 12 Oct):** **one number, 7 years**, for the whole activity log (firm entries and ExpatReady staff actions). SA05-Q4 stands; decision 11's "e.g. 2 years" is superseded; SA-11 §13 Q1 closed; the firm "keep longer" setting (SA-11 §13 Q12, US-3, BR-6 refusal path) is dropped — nothing deletes in Release 1 (append-only log, no deletion path, R13). Product wording: "Your activity log is kept for 7 years for security." No code change.

## Rule 9 pass — SA-12 (the product owner approved 12 Oct; item 4 complete)

- **Main (staff):** flag on / off (catalogue default; new modules off); kill switch (not tripped; confirm + reason); firm exceptions on the firm's account view; platform maintenance (off; **bilingual** message — SA-12 §7 corrected); banner EN / FR message + start / end. **Advanced:** banner severity (info default), dismissible; banner link dropped. **Code / deploy:** rate limit 100 req / 60 s per IP (as built); upload limits (R32); step-up 5 min. **Security settings:** fixed form, not a typed editor — staff email domains and auto-logout (two numbers) under Advanced, Owner only; password rules and invite link removed (R9-21).
- **R9-33 Flag list / detail:** plain feature name and "what Off does" first; key, owner, lifecycle under Advanced; "why is this on for this firm" as a sentence. Flag metadata **read-only in the console**, changed by release (as built; SA-12 §3.1 corrected).
- **R9-34 Disposal switch (G-Q1):** a kill-switch-eligible flag "Stop data erasure" — reuses the built kill switch, no new setting.
- **SA-12 §13 closed:** Q3 single holder + reason + audit · Q4 100 / 60 s per IP · Q5 no auto-expiry; overrides > 30 days highlighted · Q6 no record beyond the audit entry · Q8 "Temporarily unavailable" · Q9 `system/platform-config:manage` Owner only (as coded, SR-Q4(3)) · Q10 one active banner, info / warning, dismissible, no link · Q11 client government-deadline and status-expiring messages keep running · Q12 built keys kept + one key each for maintenance and banner · Q13 unsubscribe / STOP non-switchable (map §17 #4) · Q14 banners outside the template catalogue · Q15 closed by SA05-Q14 (code constant) · Q16 examples use the built keys.

- **N6 (c):** a message skipped by Guard 1 creates **no "Needs you" item** — the dashboard note is enough.
- **N6 (d):** the note is **fixed ExpatReady wording** (e.g. "Invoice reminders are waiting for your payment instructions — add them"), shown on the **dashboard** to Owner and Admins and **beside that message** on the optional automatic-messages page; **one note per missing detail**, not per client.
