Within each section the most urgent comes first, and anything tied to a government deadline sits at the top. **Empty sections are hidden.** Each count opens its full list, and the full lists stay where they are. Correction made the same day: "My work" in AG16-29, AG16-30 and AG16-36 now reads "the home screen". The AG-14 dashboards' placement stays pending (`AG-11…:60`). Not built. To apply: AG-11 (§ home screen, US-18), AG-13 (My Tasks section), AG-10, AG-06.
- **AG16-41 — Letters downloaded from the IRCC portal (F2, option C; 16 Oct).** The product owner confirmed that portal messages are PDF letters. (1) The **letter library (AG16-27 steps 3–5) also runs on any PDF uploaded to a case or dropped onto the case page**: letter type, suggested deadline, task, "tell the client". (2) **Drop on the home screen:** the case is found from the application number or UCI in the PDF ("Biometrics instruction letter — Client B, W30•••••. File here?"); if nothing matches, it asks which case. (3) **Bulk drop** of several PDFs files each one to its own case, with one summary to confirm. (4) **Forwarding** the PDF to the firm mailbox also works (forwards are unwrapped and filed, AG16-27); no new forwarding address. (5) A letter that arrives both by email and by upload is recognised as **one** letter (same PDF), so it creates one task *(changed 17 Oct, AG16-44: both copies kept; staff confirm "Same letter")*. **Not added:** a "paste portal text" box (portal content is PDFs); a browser button (depends on IRCC's page layout and terms; not practical); automatic portal login (credential and terms risk). The portal download itself stays manual; IRCC offers no connection to its portal. Not built. To apply: AG-09 (upload runs the letter library; home-screen drop), AG-10, AG-11 (home screen).
- **AG16-42 — "Quiet by default", plus telling the client (F3, option A; 17 Oct).** **Standing rule, applies to all mail, letter and payment features.** Every alert or home-screen item must pass one test: *real work for this person, which they would otherwise miss*. It uses facts ExpatReady already knows: (a) who owns the item (case officer, file owner, lead owner); (b) which mailbox the email arrived in and who reads it; (c) permissions (billing, `resolve-unmatched`, `case:assign`). No setting is needed; a solo firm automatically gets less. (1) **Filing is silent:** filed items show in Done for you and on the record, never as an alert. This **changes AG16-27(4)**: FYI government mail (status updated, linked, receipt) is filed with no notification. (2) **A home-screen item means real work;** nothing is created only to inform. (3) **Machine mail never enters Unmatched:** no-reply senders, mailing-list headers and known app senders (e-sign, booking, ads, bounces) are not brought in *(17 Oct, AG16-44: listed under "Not brought in" with [Bring these in])*. This **refines AG16-15/AG16-25** (firm mailbox "everything") and AG-10-65. Evidence: 12.6% of incoming mail was tool, ad or e-sign notifications (`inbox_mix_snapshot.md`). (4) **No duplicate alerts:** a filed client email alerts its owner only if it arrived in a mailbox the owner does not read (e.g. client → info@, owner not reading info@). This **refines AG16-25(6)**. (5) **Bulk confirm** for repetitive suggestions: "Record all N exact matches" for payments (refines AG16-30). Deadlines stay one by one. Existing rules already consistent: no escalation alert to the responsible person (AG16-22); sections shown only to permission holders and hidden when empty (AG16-40). **F3 telling the client:** "Tell the client" on a letter task (AG16-27) opens a message **pre-filled from the letter library** (a template per letter type and language, not AI) with the client's name, the deadline and the letter attached. The officer reviews and clicks Send. Messenger / Mailer deliver it to the portal and the client's preferred channel (email, or WhatsApp if connected). An optional tick, "Remind [client] until they confirm", uses the existing chase timing (AG16-12, Reminder). On AI plans "Rewrite (AI)" uses the Writer; the Send click is the approval (AG11-D2). The client sees a **"Last update" date** on their case. **Not added:** an automatic "update received from IRCC" line on the portal (it could create chasing before the firm has looked); no automatic client message on decisions or refusals. Not built. To apply: AG-10 (§3.2/3.2a filters, notifications), AG-11 (home screen, Done for you), AG-06 (bulk record), AG-12 (letter-library notes as templates), AG-15 (Last update date), SA-01/SA-05 (letter-library content per language).
- **AG16-43 — Keeping the letter library current (F4, option A; 17 Oct).** (1) **Staff pick the type** of an unrecognised letter ("Letter — type not recognised", AG16-27) as part of handling its task: from the library's list, or "Other: [free text]". (2) **A de-identified pattern goes to ExpatReady, automatically:** only the sender domain, the attachment file-name pattern (digits masked), the title or first sentence with **all names and numbers removed**, and the type chosen. Never the letter, the body, attachments or any client field. (3) **Review in Super Admin:** a "New letter patterns" list, grouped by similarity with a count of firms ("seen at 4 firms"). The Immigration SME adds or adjusts a `LetterLibraryEntry` (type, phrases, IMM form codes, deadline rule, client note per language). With AI, ExpatReady staff may draft the entry from the pattern (AG16-33). (4) **Publish** as pack content to all firms at once, with no release needed (SA-01/SA-05 pack versioning). (5) **Cadence:** weekly review at first, monthly once stable; the SME owns it. No firm setting; the terms of service state that de-identified government-template wording is shared. Removal of names and numbers must be tested against the de-identified fixtures (BUILD-CARD-STANDARD). Not built. To apply: AG-10 (unrecognised-letter type picker), SA-01/SA-05 (LetterLibraryEntry, review queue, publish), SA-07 (SME role access), SA-11 (audit of pattern submissions).
- **AG16-44 — ExpatReady never quietly judges for the firm (revisit, option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Extends AG16-31 with a general rule: **wherever ExpatReady treats two things as the same, or decides something is not important, it shows that and the firm can reverse it.** The only exception is identical technical copies. Changes: (1) **Form leads** (AG16-32(4)): no automatic merge; "Possible duplicate — [Link] [Merge] [Keep separate]" (AG-02 §3.2). (2) **Same-looking government letters** (forwarded, emailed and uploaded; AG16-27(1), AG16-41(5)): every copy is kept and filed. When a copy looks like a letter already filed: "Looks like the letter filed on [date] — [Same letter] [Different letter]". There is one task unless staff choose "Different letter". (3) **Machine mail not brought in** (AG16-42(3)): each mailbox has a **"Not brought in"** list (sender and count, last 30 days) with **[Bring these in]** per sender, so the firm can reverse it. Kept: (a) **identical copies of one email** in several mailboxes are stored once by message ID (AG-10-64), because they are the same message and not a judgement; the email shows **"Also in: client-g@"**. (b) Rule-based filing with exactly one open case (AG16-26), now with **[Move]** and **[Un-file]** on every filed email (un-file is audit-logged). (c) Silent filing of FYI updates (AG16-42). (d) Suggestions confirmed by a person (AG16-27, AG16-30). (e) Firm-enabled form-lead rules, which can be turned off per sender (AG16-32). Not built. To apply: AG-02 (§3.2 applies to form notifications), AG-10 (Not brought in list, Also in, Move/Un-file, same-letter prompt), AG-09 (letter copies).
- **AG16-45 — Office time zones and escalation by office (option A; 17 Oct).** Applies **only once a second office exists**; offices stay hidden until then (AG01-10), so solo and one-office firms are unchanged. Now: the firm time zone is pre-filled from the address's province (`AG-16…:557`); per-office hours exist but use the firm time zone (`AG-16…:507, :778`); `Office` has no time zone (`AG-01…:663`); escalation alerts everyone with `agent/case:assign` (AG16-22). Rules: (1) **`Office.timezone`** (IANA), pre-filled from the office address's province (e.g. Calgary → America/Edmonton) and editable. That office's hours, closed days and the five internal clocks (AG16-6) use it. (2) The **case's office** (`officeId`, already on records) decides which time zone and closed days apply to that case. (3) ~~**Escalation by office:** deadline and overdue alerts go to `agent/case:assign` holders **in the case's office, plus the Owner**. There is still no alert to the responsible person (AG16-22).~~ *(Dropped — AG16-51: escalation stays with everyone holding `agent/case:assign`, per AG16-22.)* (4) **Times shown on screen** use the viewer's office time zone, with the zone named when it differs, e.g. "10:00 (Calgary)". (5) Government deadlines unchanged: calendar dates never move (AG16-6). Not built. To apply: AG-01 (`Office.timezone`), AG-16 (§3.14 hours per office time zone; Offices screen), AG-11 / AG-17 (clocks and escalation recipients), AG-13.
- **AG16-46 — Closed days: suggested holidays, firm adds (option A; 17 Oct).** Amends AG16-6 without reversing it: the closed-days list is still **the firm's own and starts empty**; ExpatReady seeds nothing. Problem: with an empty list the five internal clocks (AG16-6) count holidays as working days and booking offers slots on e.g. Christmas Day; moving holidays (Good Friday, Family Day, Thanksgiving) must be looked up each year. Rules: (1) Button **"Add holidays for [province] [year]"** on the closed-days screen (province from the firm address, or the office's once offices exist, AG16-45). It shows that year's provincial and federal holidays, **pre-ticked**, labelled "Suggested — check before adding"; the firm unticks any day it works and clicks **Add**. **Nothing is saved until Add** (AG16-31, AG16-44 pattern). (2) A **"Same date every year"** tick on any closed day the firm adds itself (e.g. office anniversary), which carries it into the next year. (3) **Yearly nudge:** in December, one home-screen item for the Owner (AG16-40): "Add next year's closed days", with the same button; cleared when clicked or dismissed. No other alerts (AG16-42). (4) The holiday reference list is **pack content** maintained by ExpatReady (per province, per year, about 1 hour a year), labelled as a suggestion; the firm's saved list is never changed when the reference list changes. Engine: rules, no AI; Doer: none (a settings screen) plus the Reminder for the December item. Not built. To apply: AG-16 (§3.14 hours and closed days), AG-04 (bookable slots use the list), AG-11 (home-screen item), SA-05 or the pack-content home (holiday reference list per province).
- **AG16-47 — Automatic messages page: find and fix (option A, corrected; 17 Oct).** Now: about 59 automatic messages (SA-05 Appendix A, kind "Auto"), grouped by firing class, no search or filter (`AG-12…:234`); nothing built (`ERv2-frontend/src/features/automation/automation-page.tsx` is a 51-line route table). Rules: (1) **Groups by journey stage**, matching the SA-05 Appendix A modules in plain words: Enquiries, Bookings, Retainer and signing, Invoices, Case updates, Documents and questionnaire, Portal, Employer (LMIA). Groups are collapsed, each with a count, e.g. "Invoices · 5 · 1 off". The firing class becomes a small plain label on the row. (2) **One search box** matching the name and the trigger (e.g. "invoice", "biometrics"), the same behaviour as the Templates lists (`AG-12-42`). (3) **One filter, "Changed from default"**: any row whose choice or timing differs from ExpatReady's default, or whose wording is the firm's. A changed row shows the default beside it and **Reset to default**, the AG-16 Waiting times pattern (`AG-16…:497`). (4) **Reset on this page covers the choice and the timing only.** Resetting the wording stays in Templates ("Reset to ExpatReady's wording"), linked from the row (single home, AG16-11). (5) Groups for an extra or feature that is off **stay visible**, collapsed and read-only, e.g. "Employer (LMIA) · not on your plan", consistent with AG-12 §3.11 (`AG-12…:316`). They are not hidden. (6) **"Automatic · [message name] · Change"** on every automatic message in the client's or lead's history opens that exact row on the page. It uses the send record that already stores the template and recipe (SA-05 §3.6.8). **Not added:** a visual journey map, per-module toggles in other epics, bulk on/off, sorting options, saved views. Engine: rules, no AI; Doer: none (settings screen). Permission unchanged: `agent/template:configure`. Not built. To apply: AG-12 (§3.3, AG-12-52, AG-12-112 – 115 and a new requirement for the history link), SA-05 Appendix A (a journey-stage tag per Auto row), AG-10 (history line shows the link).
- **AG16-48 — WhatsApp: keep the firm's existing number and app (coexistence) (option A; 17 Oct).** Now: "its own WhatsApp Business account" (`AG-16…:384`, `AG-16-38`); nothing said about the phone app; provider not chosen (SA8-D31); not built (only `apps/api/infra/07-capability-seed.sql`, `platform-flag/flag.service.ts`). Pattern: HubSpot (coexistence, 180 days imported), Wati (contacts plus up to 6 months, history choice only at setup), Zoho, 360dialog. Rules: (1) **One "Connect WhatsApp" button** (Connections; `agent/integration-whatsapp:manage`, Owner and Admin) with two choices: **"Keep my current WhatsApp Business number and app"** (default, Meta coexistence via Embedded Signup) or "Use a new number". Status and reconnect follow `AG-16-15`–`19`. (2) **Checklist before connecting** *(changed — AG16-51: a plain list to read, not ticked)*: admin of the firm's Meta business account; app up to date; number used in the app 7+ days. (3) **"What changes" list** before confirming: groups and calls stay on the phone; broadcast lists, disappearing messages, view-once and live location stop for one-to-one chats; linked computers need re-linking; the app must stay in use on the phone. (4) **Past chats, asked once, stated as one-time:** ◉ Only chats with clients and leads already in ExpatReady (default) · ○ All chats from the last 6 months · ○ None. With "All", unknown numbers go to **"Not brought in"** with "Create lead" (AG16-44); nothing becomes a lead or client automatically (AG16-31). Earlier media shows as "Photo — on the phone". The phone's address book is never imported. (5) **After connecting:** new messages are filed by the mailbox rule (AG16-26): a number on exactly one record is filed, otherwise Unmatched; a new number is a possible enquiry (AG16-25). Phone replies appear in the history marked **"Sent from phone"**. A dropped connection is one home-screen item for the Owner, "WhatsApp disconnected — Reconnect" (AG16-40), with no other alerts (AG16-42). (6) **Go-live:** the chosen provider must support coexistence and Meta's current Embedded Signup version (one source: v2 retires 15 Oct 2026); exact Meta limits are checked against Meta and the provider at build time (confidence medium, third-party sources). **Not added:** address-book import, group sync, staff personal WhatsApp (plain WhatsApp cannot connect), consent changes (the "WhatsApp opt-in" warning is unchanged). Engine: rules, no AI; Doer: Messenger (sending), filing as for mail. Not built. To apply: AG-16 (§3.4 rail 4, AG-16-38), AG-10 (WhatsApp inbound filing, "Sent from phone", history import), AG-11 (home-screen item), SA-08 (before-go-live provider requirement), DEV-TASKS (provider choice).
- **AG16-49 — Client quiet hours in the client's own time zone (option A; 17 Oct).** Now: quiet hours are optional and use the firm or office time zone (`AG-10-8`, `BR-9`); the AG-10 test at `AG-10…:632` holds a staff-written email outside business hours; nothing built; contacts have no time zone (only the user's `timezone`, `prisma/models/identity-auth.prisma:32`). Pattern: HubSpot and Klaviyo (recipient time zone), Intercom (quiet hours), US texting rules (8 am–9 pm recipient local), Jane and Cliniko. Rules: (1) **Automatic non-urgent messages** (chases, reminders, follow-ups, marketing, timed class 3) send only **08:00–20:00 in the client's local time**; outside it they wait until 08:00. E.g. a chase due at midnight Dubai time arrives at 08:00 Dubai time. (2) **Always sent at once:** class 1 direct responses (booking confirmed, payment receipt, signing link, password and security emails), **any message a staff member writes and sends**, and in-window WhatsApp replies (`AG-10-41`). (3) **Client time zone filled automatically:** from the address (country and province or state), else the phone's country code, else the case's office time zone (AG16-45). Shown on the record as "Time zone: Dubai (auto)" and editable. New field `Contact.timezone` (IANA, nullable, auto-filled). (4) **Composer cue:** "It's 23:40 for Client B (Dubai)" plus one-click **"Send at 08:00 their time"** using the existing scheduled send (`AG-10-8`); a plain Send still sends at once (Rule 7: warn, never block). (5) ~~**One Advanced line:** "Automatic messages reach clients between 08:00 and 20:00 their time", editable.~~ *(Dropped — AG16-51: the window is fixed.)* Fixes: `AG-10-8` and `BR-9` use the client's time zone; the `AG-10…:632` test is changed so a staff-written message is not held. **Not added:** weekend rules, per-client quiet hours, smart send time. Engine: rules, no AI; Doers: Mailer, Messenger, Reminder. Not built. To apply: AG-10 (`AG-10-8`, `AG-10-17`, `BR-9`, `BR-31`, tests), AG-07 / AG-02 (`Contact.timezone` on the record), AG-16 (Advanced line), AG-11 (sending engine respects the window).
- Earlier queue: ~~office timezone and office-scoped escalation~~ (AG16-45); ~~closed-days suggestions~~ (AG16-46); ~~Automatic messages grouping~~ (AG16-47); ~~WhatsApp coexistence~~ (AG16-48); ~~client quiet hours~~ (AG16-49); ~~unassigned overdue Needs-you items~~ (AG16-50).
- **AG16-50 — Government-deadline reminder on a case with no one assigned goes to the Owner (option A′, lean; 17 Oct).** Closes AG-16 §13 Q19 and AG-11 Q34. Already covered, unchanged: the one-click handover with a pre-selected colleague (AG1-D5); skipped work goes to the Unassigned lists with a home-screen badge for anyone who can reassign work (AG1-D7); cases may have no owner (AG7-D6); unassigned items are visible at once to `agent/case:assign` holders (`AG-16…:483`). The only gap: with Escalation off, a government-deadline reminder on a case with **no one assigned** reached no one (AG16-22: "only the responsible person is reminded"). Rule: such a reminder goes to the **Owner**, on the same schedule (2 days before and on the day). With Escalation on, nothing changes, and there is no duplicate alert to the Owner (AG16-22). **Not added:** a home-screen "No one assigned" item, "Take it", a fallback for leads, tasks or other Needs-you items, a fallback-person setting. Engine: rules; Doer: Minder. Not built. To apply: AG-11 (deadline reminder recipients; close Q34), AG-16 (§3.8, close Q19).
- **AG16-51 — Three trims to avoid over-building (the product owner, 17 Oct).** After an over-engineering check of AG16-24 – AG16-50 (test: does it push an alert or only show something?). (1) **AG16-48:** the pre-connection checklist is a **plain list to read**, not ticked item by item; Meta's sign-up checks these anyway. (2) **AG16-49:** the **08:00–20:00 window is fixed**; the editable Advanced line is dropped. (3) **AG16-45:** **escalation by office is dropped**; escalation goes to everyone with `agent/case:assign`, as AG16-22 says. Office time zones, the case's office driving clocks, and times shown with the zone named all stay. Kept as checked: "Waiting on us" and "Overdue by person" are lists, not alerts; the pushed alerts are only government-deadline reminders, Escalation (switchable), a person's own "Remind me if no reply", "WhatsApp disconnected" and the December closed-days item.
- **AG16-52 — Connecting one's own mailbox or calendar needs no permission (option A; 17 Oct).** Closes AG-04 §13 Q13, SA-06 Open Question 21 and SA6-X15. Now: `agent/integration-calendar:create` is seeded for Owner and Admin only (`AG-04…:744`), yet everyone connects their own email and calendar in My account (AG16-15, AG16-18, AG16-25). Rule: **any signed-in staff member may connect, change or disconnect their own mailbox and own calendar** in My account, with no permission key, like changing their own password (pattern: SA-06's own-preferences key, `SA-06…:1424`; HubSpot, Clio, Pipedrive, Calendly). **Firm-level connections stay restricted:** firm mailbox and WhatsApp (Owner and Admin), payment account (Owner only, `agent/payment-rail:connect`), the firm's video account (see the next video decision). Control stays with the firm: a personal mailbox brings in only what the person chooses (AG16-25); Team shows "Email: connected / Calendar: connected" per person; deactivation stops syncing (AG16-37). Knock-on: ~~`agent/integration-calendar:create` keeps only the firm's video account (rewording in AG-04 §10 and SA-06), pending the video decision~~ *(superseded by AG16-53: the key is withdrawn)*. No new key. Not built.
- **AG16-53 — Video links are per person; no firm video account (option A; 17 Oct).** Closes AG-16 §13 Q17. Now: AG-04 §3.6 (`AG-04…:285-290`) has three tiers: an in-house video room ("may be a thin redirect for the demo, full WebRTC as a roadmap item"), the **firm's** own Zoom / Meet / Teams account in Settings → Connections, and a manual link; none built. Problem: Meet and Teams links are created on the host's own calendar event, and Zoom licences are per host, so one firm account puts meetings under the wrong host and clashes on overlapping bookings. Pattern: Calendly, Cal.com, HubSpot Meetings, Clio Scheduler, Jane (per person). Rules: (1) **Video lives in My account**; Settings → Connections has no video card. Each video booking gets its link in this order: (a) **Meet or Teams automatically** from the host's connected Google or Microsoft calendar (AG16-18), no extra connection; (b) **"Connect Zoom"** in My account, optional, per person; (c) **"My meeting link"**, one saved link (e.g. a Zoom personal room) used for every video booking; (d) **paste a link** on one booking, always available (as today). (2) **No firm video account.** `agent/integration-calendar:create` is **withdrawn** (after AG16-52 it covers nothing); connecting one's own Zoom needs no permission (AG16-52). (3) **The in-house video room (tier 1) is not in Release 1** (the product owner accepted with option A, 17 Oct; raised explicitly per the Release 1 rule; nothing built; can be revisited). (4) Release 1: all of (a)–(d). Go-live item: Zoom Marketplace app approval for ExpatReady. Engine: rules; Doer: Scheduler. Not built. To apply: AG-04 (§3.6 rewritten, AG-4-29, §3.9, §10 key withdrawn, §13), AG-16 (Connections card removed, My account → Video, close Q17), SA-06 (key withdrawn), SA-08 (Zoom on the provider list and before-go-live list), DEV-TASKS (Zoom app approval).
- **AG16-54 — 25 consistency fixes after the 17 Oct write (Group 1, approved as a batch; the product owner, 17 Oct).** No new choice; text is aligned to decisions already made. Source list: `scratchpad/REVIEW-QUEUE-17oct.md` Group 1. (1) SA-05 "optional on/off page" (§3.2.2, SA-5-89) and "AI extra" / "auto-nudge extra" (A.8 #3, A.11 #2–3) → AG16-11 / AG16-34. (2) AG-7-94 and AG-11-110 → "ExpatReady seeds no closed days; it offers a suggested list" (AG16-46). (3) AG-17-60: delete the clause routing to the removed notification matrix (AG16-14). (4) AG-01 §7 FirmConfig "business-hours profile" and the pointer to withdrawn AG-1-73 → delete. (5) SA 00-Overview SA-5 row → AG16-11. (6) SA3-X12 and SA8X25 → Pending, closed after the fix pass. (7) Rule 7: regulator and statute names in AG-04 product text (lines 151, 251, 491, 721, 927, 948, 1028) → plain words; AG-06 §3.5 and footer are spec explanations and stay. (8) One mailbox entity, `MailboxConnection` (home AG-10); AG-16 `FirmConnection` refers to it for mailboxes. (9) Keys: `agent/communication:connect-mailbox` withdrawn for one's own mailbox (AG16-52); `agent/integration-mail-receive:manage` and `agent/integration-smtp:manage` reworded as "firm mailbox"; `agent/support-ticket:create` / `:list` put into capability 3 (SA-6-47). (10) Application-number and UCI patterns are pack content, not code (AG17-D1). (11) AI-read form-notification fields appear on the lead as "Suggested details" (AG16-33). (12) A low-confidence or AI-only Sorter match goes to Unmatched only, not also Needs you (AG16-42). (13) Rules read a PDF's text layer; the Reader is used only for scans (AG16-26 wording corrected; BUILD-CARD-STANDARD worked example fixed). (14) "Remind me if no reply" is a home-screen item, not a pushed alert (AG16-51 list corrected). (15) "Add to case" uses the built `PartyRelationship` (`client-spine.prisma:125`) plus an optional `caseId`; no new `CaseRelatedContact` table (built stays). (16) AG-07 adds `Case.officeId` (default: the client's office) and `Case.clientLastUpdateAt` (AG-15-54 knock-on). (17) The "Employer (LMIA)" group on the Automatic messages page is always visible (corporate is on every plan, AG17-D35); read-only only if ExpatReady switches it off. (18) AG17-D56 and AG16-50 cover different events; both kept, no change. (19) AG-09 is the home of letter upload, letter copies and attachment-fulfils-request; AG-10-72, -75 and -80 become one-line pointers; AG-10-71 keeps the email path only. (20) AG-16 keeps the Escalation switch and key and points to AG-11 for recipients (§3.8, AG-16-32, BR-15, §9, US-10). (21) SA-11: Move and Un-file audited as one event type; pattern-submission audit holds pattern id and type only; mail-related audit entries hold ids only, never content. (22) The Team list column "Email: connected / Calendar: connected" has its home in AG-01. (23) SA-08: provider list in one home (SA8-D31 + SA-8-57), Gmail routes in one home (SA-8-69), pointers elsewhere; the Nylas fallback is one line in the before-go-live list, not a card. (24) In this map, "SME" in AG16-43 means the Content publisher (SA-07 has no SME role). (25) AG16-48 (6): build on Meta's current Embedded Signup version (the "v2 retires 15 Oct 2026" note is past). To apply: in the fix pass, together with the Group 2 decisions.
- **AG16-55 — Writers' recommended defaults accepted for the detail questions (Group 3; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Each open question raised in the 17 Oct versions is closed with the default its writer recommended in that file's open-questions list, citing AG16-55: AG-01 Q13–16 · AG-02 Q21–28 · AG-04 Q14–18 · AG-06 items 14–21 · AG-07 Q26–33 · AG-09 Q16–20 · AG-10 Q22–35 · AG-11 Q36–52 · AG-12 Q12–16 · AG-13 Q13–16 · AG-15 Q12 · AG-16 Q20–25 · AG-17 items 5–8 · AG-18 Q24 · SA-01 SA1-Q5–15 · SA-03 Q9–11 · SA-05 N11–17 · SA-06 OQ22–28 · SA-07 OQ12–13 · SA-08 Q6–11 · SA-11 Q21–22. **Exceptions:** a question with no stated default stays open and is raised to the product owner; where a default overlaps a Group 2 decision (`REVIEW-QUEUE-17oct.md`), the Group 2 decision wins; a default that would move a day-to-day feature to Release 2 is not accepted and is raised to the product owner (Release 1 rule). To apply: in the fix pass.
- **AG16-56 — When automatic messages send, by what their timer counts (option A′; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #1 (`REVIEW-QUEUE-17oct.md`; SA-05 N12). Problem: under AG16-49 a held "Complete your payment" (10 minutes into a 30-minute hold) or a "1 hour before" booking reminder (`AG-04…:318`, offsets 24h and 1h) could arrive after the hold or the meeting for a client abroad; the government deadline countdown, classed 1 (`AG-12…:172`), would send at 03:00 client time; day-based timers had no defined send time. Rule (engine only; no setting, no label, no UI): (1) **Messages counted in days** (chases, follow-ups, invoice and signing reminders, the deadline countdown) send at **09:00 in the client's time zone on the due day**. E.g. a passport chase due 20 Oct goes at 09:00 Dubai time on 20 Oct. (2) **Messages tied to a time the client booked or a payment hold** (booking reminders, "Complete your payment", "Payment expired") send **at the exact time, any hour**. E.g. a call at 23:00 Manila time → the 1-hour reminder at 22:00 Manila time. (3) **Any other automatic message** follows the 08:00–20:00 client-time window of AG16-49. (4) Unchanged: class 1 direct responses and staff-written messages send at once. The deadline countdown is no longer exempt as class 1; rule (1) governs it. Pattern: HubSpot, Klaviyo (recipient time zone), Calendly (reminders at the exact offset). Engine: rules; Doers: Reminder, Mailer, Messenger, Scheduler. Not built. Closes SA-05 N12. To apply: AG-10 (sending window card, `AG-10-8`, `BR-9`), AG-11 (sending engine), AG-04 (booking reminders and payment hold send at exact time), AG-12 (countdown class label), SA-05 (N12 closed; A.2 #7, #8, A.5 #15).
- **AG16-57 — Two places for messages that were not filed: Unmatched and "Not brought in" (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #2. Problem: three places (`AG-10…:293, :528, :548`); AG16-48 sent unknown numbers from imported WhatsApp chats to "Not brought in" with "Create lead", contradicting AG-2-62 (unknown WhatsApp number → Unmatched) and turning the machine-mail log into a second lead inbox; the personal "Show me possible new enquiries" list duplicated Unmatched with privacy as the only difference. Rules: (1) **Unmatched is the one to-do list for a person who wrote and was not filed:** firm-mailbox mail and WhatsApp (new and imported). Items from a **personal** mailbox appear **only to that person**, marked **"Only you"**; the switch (renamed as needed) stays per personal mailbox, **off by default**. **"Create lead" lives only in Unmatched.** (2) **"Not brought in" is machine mail only:** per-mailbox log, sender and count, last 30 days, "Bring these in"; no "Create lead". (3) Imported past WhatsApp chats from unknown numbers go to Unmatched marked **"Past chat"** and do not add to the home-screen count *(only while the "All chats" import choice exists — see Group 2 #18)*. (4) **Own items always visible:** every person sees their own "Only you" items whatever their role; `agent/communication:resolve-unmatched` governs firm items only (who may file: Group 2 #9). (5) **Once only:** an email in both a firm mailbox and a personal mailbox shows once, as the firm item (message-ID dedupe, AG-10-64). (6) **Leaving:** a person's "Only you" items are removed when their connection ends (AG16-37). (7) **Counts:** each person's home-screen Unmatched count includes their own private items only. Pattern: HubSpot (unlinked emails from a personal inbox visible only to that user in the same view). Supersedes AG16-25(4)'s separate list and AG16-48(4)'s "Not brought in" routing. Engine: rules; Doer: Mailer, Messenger; Sorter unchanged (AG16-54 #12). Not built. To apply: AG-10 (AG-10-65, AG-10-68, AG-10-82, AG-10-84, BR-34, §3.2a, tests), AG-16 (AG-16-38 card rule 4, My account email line), AG-02 (AG-2-70 pointer), AG-11 (home-screen Unmatched count), SA-06 (key scope note).
- **AG16-58 — Reply address and a From line in the composer (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #3; closes AG-10 open question 24 (`AG-10…:1485`). Conflict: AG16-15 (a person's message goes from their own mailbox) vs AG16-28(3) (a reply goes from the address the email arrived at). Rules: (1) **A reply goes from the address the email arrived at.** E.g. a client writes to info@; Client G, who has his own Gmail connected, replies → it goes from info@, shown as "Client G, Maple Immigration" with his signature; it lands in info@'s Sent folder; the client's next email returns to info@. A client who wrote to client-g@ gets the reply from client-g@. (2) **A new message** is unchanged (AG16-15): own mailbox if connected, else main firm mailbox, else shared sending. (3) **From line in the composer:** shows the sending address, pre-set by (1)–(2); the person may change it for one email to their own address or a firm mailbox. (4) **Arrived-at mailbox disconnected:** fall back to the main firm mailbox, then shared sending; the From line shows the address used. (5) An email received in both a firm and a personal mailbox is a firm item (AG16-57), so the reply goes from the firm address. Why: firm conversations stay in firm mailboxes, visible when staff are away and unbroken when they leave (23 bounces for one deleted staff address in 14 days, `inbox_mix_snapshot.md:44`). Pattern: Gmail and Outlook (From line), Front, Help Scout. Who may send from a firm mailbox follows Group 2 #9. Engine: rules; Doer: Mailer. Not built. To apply: AG-10 (AG-10-63, BR-33, §3.2a, composer, card; close Q24), AG-16 (pointer).
- **AG16-59 — A government letter records its case step; one event, one client message (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #4; closes SA-05 N15. Problem: the same event had two paths: the letter task's "Tell the client" (AG16-42, `AG-10-73`, SA-05 A.8a) and recording the government step on the case (`AG-07…:428, :441`, `CaseMilestone`) with its class-2 box (SA-05 A.5 #6, #7, #10, #11, #13); the client could get two messages and the officer typed the event twice. Rules: (1) **Confirming a letter's suggested deadline also records the matching case step** for the person the letter names, e.g. "Biometrics requested, 2 Nov, due 13 Nov". Each letter type in the letter library names its step (pack content, SA-01); a type with no step records none. (2) **The client message for that step is the letter note** via "Tell the client", with the letter attached; the class-2 box is **not offered** for a step recorded from a letter. (3) **A step recorded by hand without a letter** works as today (class-2 box offered). (4) **Reverse order:** if the step was recorded by hand and the client told, "Tell the client" still appears on the letter task with "Client B was told about this on 12 Oct"; nothing hidden or blocked. (5) For a step recorded from a letter, the date editor's "offer to set a reminder" (`AG-07…:441`) is not repeated; the letter's deadline already exists. (6) Dismissing the letter or not confirming records nothing and sends nothing. (7) **Safeguard (the product owner, 17 Oct):** sending via "Tell the client" completes the letter task; "Mark done" without sending asks "Client B hasn't been told about this letter. [Tell the client] [Done without telling]" — warns, never blocks; no extra click in the normal case. The product owner's principle (17 Oct): **better two notices than a missed one**; nothing here suppresses a message without a visible alternative. Whether step and deadline become one table stays Group 2 #15. Engine: rules; Doers: Minder, Tasker, Messenger. Not built. To apply: AG-07 (AG-7-32, step rail, card), AG-09 (AG-9-60 step 9), AG-10 (AG-10-73), SA-01 (letter type → step), SA-05 (N15 closed; A.5 / A.8a notes), AG-12 (class-2 note).
- **AG16-60 — Standing rule: a duplicate beats a miss (the product owner, 17 Oct).** **Applies everywhere, alongside AG16-31, -33, -42, -44.** Where something could be missed that affects a **client, a deadline, money or a broken connection**, ExpatReady accepts duplicate work or a second notice rather than risk the miss. Purely informational items stay quiet (AG16-42). Any de-duplication or suppression must leave a visible alternative (as AG16-59 does). The product owner: "where chances are there, we will accept duplicate work rather than missing something trivial." Checked against AG16-56 – AG16-59: each keeps a safety net. Effect on the queue: Group 2 #7 (silent only for byte-identical PDFs), #10 (keep the overlapping no-reply signals), #22 (WhatsApp failure: home-screen item and email). To apply: glossary standing rules (Agent 99-Glossary), WRITER-BRIEF §1, BUILD-CARD-STANDARD field 10 (Safety).
- **AG16-61 — Due date and visibility of the task made from a government letter (option A+; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #5; closes AG-11 Q46 and AG-10 Q28 (calendar or working days). Problem: the task had no due date, and My Tasks shows only due today or overdue (`AG-11…:844`), so the task would never appear; due on the government deadline would hide it for weeks. Rules: (1) **Due 2 working days after the letter is filed, never later than the government deadline** (suggested or confirmed). Working days skip weekends and the firm's closed days. E.g. filed Monday, deadline in 30 days → due Wednesday; filed Friday → Tuesday; uploaded late with deadline tomorrow → tomorrow; no deadline → 2 working days. (2) **Visible at once (AG16-60):** a letter task appears in the officer's home-screen My Tasks from creation, shown "due Wed", not only when due, and stays until done (with the AG16-59 safeguard). (3) The task date does not move when the deadline is later changed. (4) No one assigned → Unassigned list (AG1-D7); deadline reminders unchanged and separate (AG16-50). (5) **"Make task" from an email (AG16-36) is due in 2 working days.** Engine: rules; Doer: Tasker. Not built. To apply: AG-11 (My Tasks rule, AG-11-122, close Q46), AG-10 (AG-10-71, AG-10-79, close Q28), AG-09 (AG-9-60 step 9), AG-13 (task due date).
- **AG16-62 — Unrecognised-letter pattern: no text leaves the firm (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #6; **changes AG16-43(1)–(2)**; closes SA1-Q8 and SA1-Q9. Problem: the masked first sentence (`SA-01…:610-616`) masks only names on the case record, so other names (a sponsor not in ExpatReady, an officer, parts of an address) could reach a list ExpatReady staff see across firms; and one sentence never removes the need for a real sample, since new types are written only from de-identified samples (`SA-01…:360`, SA1-Q5). Rules: (1) A pattern submission holds exactly **three content fields:** sender domain (`cic.gc.ca`), **file-name pattern** with digit runs masked **except public form codes** matching `IMM\s?\d{4}[A-Z]?` (e.g. `IMM5756.pdf` kept; `W300000000.pdf` → `W#.pdf`), and the **type chosen** (library code or `OTHER`). **No title, sentence, subject, body or attachment.** (2) **"Other: [free text]"** stays with the firm as the document's label; only `OTHER` is sent. (3) The "New letter patterns" list shows type, file-name clue and firm count; the Content publisher writes new entries from de-identified samples collected with a design-partner firm (SA1-Q5 path). (4) Removed: the name masker, its fail-closed rule and its fixtures. Engine: rules; Doer: Tasker. Not built. To apply: SA-01 (SA1-D21, SA-1-71 card, SA1-Q8, Q9), AG-10 (picker), SA-11 (audit holds id and type only, already AG16-54 #21).
- **AG16-63 — Design checks every requirement must pass (the product owner's review principles, 14–17 Oct).** **Standing; applies to every epic, every writer and every build card.** Recorded so the concerns the product owner raised in review become requirements, not conversation. (1) **Not over-engineered, not over-monitored** (the product owner, 17 Oct: "are you sure we are not over-engineering and over-monitoring"): the AG16-51 test — does it push an alert, or only show something? Prefer showing; say plainly whether it is must-have or good-to-have; no setting where a fixed default works (Rule 9). (2) **Holistic, not a patch** (the product owner, 17 Oct: "think holistically and practical solution"; "hope you are not doing any recommendation superficially"): fix the cause, not the symptom (e.g. AG16-56 decides by what a timer counts instead of listing exceptions). Before a requirement is final it is stress-tested for knock-ons in other epics, permissions, a person leaving, counts and badges, links to other decisions, and how much gets built. (3) **A duplicate beats a miss** (AG16-60). (4) **No extra work for staff** (the product owner, 17 Oct: "will it create too much work on case officer side"): a requirement states the clicks it adds or removes for the person doing it; a change that adds routine clicks needs a reason. (5) **Best practice across SaaS** (the product owner, 16 Oct): name the pattern used by established products. (6) **ExpatReady suggests, the firm decides** (AG16-31) and **warn, never block** (Rule 7). To apply: WRITER-BRIEF §1 and §3 (writer checks), BUILD-CARD-STANDARD (field 10 Safety: AG16-60 line; new line in field 12 or 13: clicks added or removed), Agent 99-Glossary and SA 99-Glossary standing rules; the full audit checks every card against (1)–(4).
- **AG16-64 — Letter copies: same person required; a look-alike gets its own task until answered (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #7; closes AG-09 Q19 (`AG-09…:1105`); **changes AG-9-61 rule 3** (`AG-09…:686`). Problem (found by stress test): a look-alike got no task and no deadline suggestion until answered; in a family case the spouse's biometrics letter (same type, date and application number as the principal's) would look like the principal's and, if nobody answered, its deadline would be missed (breaks AG16-60). Rules: (1) **Identical bytes** on the same case: unchanged — stored once, "Also received … by email (client-g@)", no prompt, no extra task. (2) **"Looks like"** = same case, same recognised letter type, same letter date, same application number **and the same person** (UCI, else name; `CaseApplicant` principal / spouse / dependant). A different person's letter is never a look-alike and gets its own task at once. (3) **A look-alike gets its own task and deadline suggestion** (AG16-60), marked "Possible copy of the 14 Oct letter · [Same letter] [Different letter]". **Same letter** → its task and suggestion close in that click and it is listed as a copy; **Different letter** → it stays. (4) Unanswered → both tasks stay. (5) Unrecognised type → identical-bytes check only (unchanged). Clicks: one in the rare look-alike case (portal download plus emailed copy). Engine: rules; Doer: Tasker, Minder. Not built. To apply: AG-09 (AG-9-61 card rules 2–4, tests, Q19), AG-10 (AG-10-72 pointer).
- **AG16-65 — Form-notification rule matches sender + subject pattern, learned and shown (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #8; closes AG-02 Q23 (`AG-02…:712`). Problem: the sample firm's website form emails each enquiry from info@ to info@ (64 in 14 days, `AG-02…:707`); a sender-only rule would turn every self-sent staff email into a lead. Rules: (1) The offer groups repeated Unmatched items by **sender + subject line (digit runs ignored)** and shows exactly what it will match: "From info@ with subject 'New enquiry from website' — 12 so far. Always create leads from these?" No setup by the firm. (2) **Each form gets its own rule** (contact form, assessment form, chat transcripts differ by subject), so each lead gets the right source. (3) **Anything that does not match stays in Unmatched** (AG16-57), visible; a changed subject after a website redesign lands there and the offer reappears (no miss, AG16-60). (4) Unchanged: no email and no phone → no lead (AG-2-67 AI-OFF rule); spam leads removed with bulk delete (AG16-21); AI-read fields shown as "Suggested details" (AG16-54 #11). Clicks: one Yes per form, once. Pattern: Zapier Email Parser, Mailparser (sender + subject filters). Engine: rules (+ Sorter optional); Doer: Mailer, Tasker. Not built. To apply: AG-02 (AG-2-67 card rules 1–2, Q23), AG-10 (AG-10-77 offer text), AG-16 (rule list shows sender + subject).
- **AG16-66 — Limited roles and their own mailbox: automatic filing for all, hand actions by role (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #9. Rules: (1) **Any staff member may connect their own mailbox** (AG16-52 stands), including Read-only and Billing / Finance (`SA-06…:32`). (2) **Automatic filing works for every role:** their email with known clients and leads is filed to those records; it is a record of communication, not a change by the user (AG16-60). E.g. Client P (Read-only) emails Client B from Gmail about a bank letter → it appears in Client B's history. (3) **Hand actions follow the role's normal permissions:** File to client, Create lead, Move / Un-file, and sending from ExpatReady or a firm mailbox. Without the permission the user still sees their own "Only you" items (AG16-57(4)), with the buttons greyed and the reason "Your role can't file emails — ask a colleague." (4) Who can read filed emails follows normal case visibility. Nothing new to build beyond the reason line; no clicks added. Engine: rules; Doer: Mailer. To apply: AG-10 (AG-10-65, AG-10-68, roles note), SA-06 (note: own-mailbox filing is not a permission; actions keep their keys).
- **AG16-67 — "No reply" signals kept as layers; "Waiting on us" covers every client conversation (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #10; closes AG-10 Q34 (WhatsApp threads). Checked: the four signals are not duplicates — (1) Waiting on us (we owe the client; owner's home screen after 2 working days), (2) Remind me if no reply (the client owes us; the person who asked), (3) lead owner alert (AG-2-69; instant, only when the mail arrived in a mailbox the owner does not read), (4) Overdue by person (same items as 1, for the Owner) — opposite directions or different people; kept per AG16-60, shown together in the home-screen section "Waiting on us · No reply" (`AG-11…:572`). Gap found: Waiting on us covered firm-mailbox threads only (`AG-10…:915`), so an unanswered client email in a personal mailbox, or a WhatsApp message, showed nowhere. Rules: (1) Keep signals 1–4 unchanged. (2) **"Waiting on us" applies to every filed conversation with a client or lead: firm mailbox, personal mailbox and WhatsApp.** Owner = the record's owner (case officer, file owner, lead owner); same 2-working-day rule and "No reply needed". (3) A reply sent outside ExpatReady (e.g. from Gmail) is synced and resets it. (4) A personal-mailbox thread on the Owner's list shows subject and age; the email is already filed and follows normal case visibility. No new alert. Engine: rules; Doer: Mailer, Messenger. Not built. To apply: AG-10 (AG-10-76 card rule 3 and Access line; Q34), AG-13 (AG-13-68), AG-11 (home-screen section source).
- **AG16-68 — Payment notices: amount-only is a candidate; "Record all" covers invoice-number matches only (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #11; closes AG-06 Q16 (`AG-06…:874`). Problem: an amount-only match (PN-04, `AG-06…:487`) was pre-selected; recording it on the wrong client marks that client paid and silences their overdue reminders (a money miss, AG16-60), and "Record all" could do it to several at once. Rules: (1) **Amount-only = a candidate, never pre-selected:** "Amount matches INV-0060 (Client A Shah) — check the payer"; the person picks it, then records (one extra click, only on these rows). (2) **"Record all N exact matches" = step-1 matches only** (invoice number found in the message and amount equals the balance); payer-name and remembered-payer matches are recorded one by one. (3) Unchanged: nothing recorded without a click (BR-20); several candidates → none pre-selected; more than the balance refused; different currency → no suggestion; every recognised notice stays in "Payments to record" until recorded or "Not a client payment". Engine: rules (+ Sorter optional); Doer: Biller. Not built. To apply: AG-06 (AG-6-50 – 56 card rules 6, 7, 10; AG-6-54; PN-04 expected result; Q16).
- **AG16-69 — Evidence before build for payment notices (the product owner, 17 Oct).** Follows AG16-68: the decision is high confidence by logic; recognition and matching are medium until tested on real data (no real Interac or bank samples in `research_notes/`; AG-06 fixtures are synthetic, `AG-06…:483`). (1) **Before build:** collect de-identified real samples of Interac e-Transfer notices and the deposit alerts of the design-partner firm's banks; `PaymentNoticeFormat` entries are written only from real samples, never from memory (same rule as SA1-Q5). Owner: the product owner with the design-partner firm. (2) **Invoice line (closes AG-06 Q21, `AG-06…:879`):** when the firm accepts e-Transfer, every invoice, payment reminder and the portal Pay screen show "Interac message: INV-0042 [Copy]" (the invoice's own number). (3) **Go-live test:** replay the partner firm's real 14 days of notices (30 Interac, 22 bank alerts, `inbox_mix_snapshot.md`) through the matcher. Pass: no wrong invoice pre-selected. Measured: share matched on invoice number. One-off test, not a product feature. To apply: DEV-TASKS (items 1 and 3 as before-build / go-live), AG-06 (AG-6-55, Q21, fixtures note), SA-05 (invoice and reminder wording line).
- **AG16-70 — Google from day one; send-only route dropped (option A′; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #12; **changes AG16-37(3)**. The product owner: "I need Google — we can initiate the process now — we have more than 12 weeks." Facts: Google verification "can potentially take several weeks"; CASA Tier 2 is a scan of the running app, 1–3 weeks plus fixes (`google_microsoft_constraints.md:18-21`); Google's verification asks to see the feature in use (Google's standard requirement; confirm at application). So the clock starts when the Gmail connection works, not today. Rules: (1) **"Continue with Google" (read, file, send) is a launch requirement.** (2) **Start now:** Google Cloud project, OAuth consent screen, domain and brand verification, privacy policy and scope justification. (3) **Build order:** the Gmail connection is built first among mail features and submitted for verification and CASA **at least 10 weeks before launch**. (4) **Send-only route (`gmail.send`) dropped:** it cannot file mail, adds a half-connected state and a forced reconnect; verification for sending is the same request. (5) **Pilot firms** use "Continue with Google" before approval within Google's 100-user allowance for unverified apps (warning screen, `:25`). (6) **Always available:** "Other provider (IMAP/SMTP)" with an app password, with a guided screen. (7) **Trigger:** if approval has not arrived **6 weeks before launch**, the product owner decides between Nylas and launching with the app-password route. Microsoft is unaffected (free publisher verification + firm admin approval, AG16-37). To apply: SA-08 (SA8-D43, SA-8-68, SA-8-69 cards), AG-10 (AG-10-66, AG-10-67), AG-16 (Connections), DEV-TASKS (top: start Google setup now; Gmail connection first; submit by launch − 10 weeks; decision at launch − 6 weeks).
- **AG16-71 — Firm mailbox fields: drop office link, keep Group addresses (option A, trimmed; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #13; **changes AG16-28(5)–(6)**. (1) **`MailboxConnection.officeId` dropped:** nothing uses it; "via toronto@" on Unmatched items already tells staff which office's mail it is; add it when office routing is decided. (2) **`aliases[]` kept, renamed "Group addresses"** under Advanced: "Mail sent to info@ reaches us through staff mailboxes." Why: many small Google Workspace firms make info@ a Google Group that copies mail into members' personal inboxes (`google_microsoft_constraints.md:40, :49`); without it those copies follow personal-mailbox rules and new enquiries to info@ would be ignored (AG16-60). Mail to a listed address follows firm-mailbox rules (Unmatched, Create lead, Waiting on us), is stored once (message-ID), and replies go from that address (AG16-58; needs "send as" in Gmail, else the From line shows the fallback). (3) **Trimmed (over-engineering check, 17 Oct):** no automatic detection or offer; one hint line on the Connect firm mailbox screen: "Is info@ a Google Group? Add it as a group address." Header reliability for Group delivery tested at build. Engine: rules; Doer: Mailer. Not built. To apply: AG-10 (`MailboxConnection`, AG-10-62/63 card), AG-16 (Connections → Advanced).
- **AG16-72 — Client time zone from the phone code only for single-zone countries (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #14; **refines AG16-49(3)**. Order: (1) address (country and province or state); (2) **phone country code, only when that country has exactly one time zone** (e.g. +91 India, +971 UAE, +63 Philippines, +234 Nigeria); codes covering several zones (+1, +7, +61 and others) are skipped; (3) the case's office time zone. E.g. Dubai client with no address and phone +971 → Asia/Dubai; Vancouver client with no address and phone +1 → office time zone. The country-to-zone table comes from a standard library, not custom logic. Shown as "Time zone: Dubai (auto)", editable (unchanged). Engine: rules; no Doer. Not built. To apply: AG-07 (`Contact.timezone` card), AG-10 (sending window card rule 4).
- **AG16-73 — One record per government step with its deadline; UCI as a canonical answer (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #15; closes AG-07 Q26 (`AG-07…:1347`). (1) **Deadline lives on the government step:** `GovernmentDeadline` (`AG-07…:962`) is merged into the step record (`CaseMilestone`, `AG-07…:967`), which gains optional due-date fields: `dueDate`, `dueStatus` (SUGGESTED | CONFIRMED), source letter / document, rule text, source sentence, anchor, confirmed by and when. Reminders, the home screen and the countdown read this one record. Why: two copies of one fact can disagree (a deadline extended in one place, reminders reading the other) — a miss risk under AG16-60. A deadline with no pack step is a step of type **"Other government deadline"** with a name. Family cases: one step per person (AG16-59), so one deadline per person. Both tables were new (`AG-07…:1256`); less to build. (2) **UCI = a canonical answer on the person** (built `ClientCanonicalAnswer`, `client-spine.prisma:245`): the single source for forms, the mail matcher and the case screen; corrections keep history (supersede chain); **the submission keeps a snapshot** of the UCI sent; build note: index the UCI for the mail matcher's lookup. Not a profile field (`PartyProfile` is display-only, "NOT canonical answers", `client-spine.prisma:97-99`). Application and provincial file numbers stay as lists on the case (AG-7-95). *(Application numbers: superseded by AG07-D26(1), 18 Oct — each sits on its own filing. Provincial file numbers unchanged.)* Engine: rules; Doer: Minder. Not built. To apply: AG-07 (AG-7-95, AG-7-96 cards, §7 data, Q26), AG-10 (matcher reads UCI), AG-11 (deadline source), AG-08 (forms read the UCI canonical field), AG-09 (AG-9-60 step).
- **AG16-74 — Translate and Summarise in the staff member's language, saved (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #16; **refines AG16-39(1)**; closes AG-10 Q30 (`AG-10…:1491`). (1) Translate and Summarise use the **staff member's interface language** (no setting); e.g. a Montréal officer using French reads a Spanish email in French. (2) The Translate button is **hidden when the email is already in that language**. (3) **Saved with the email** the first time it is asked for, per target language (next colleague sees it at once; no second AI call; same wording for all); deleted with the email; a summary is regenerated only when a new message arrives in the thread. (4) Unchanged: original always visible; marked "Translated by AI"; staff only, never sent; hidden without AI on the plan (AG16-33, AG16-34). Engine: AI; Doer: Writer. AI OFF: buttons not shown. Not built. To apply: AG-10 (AG-10-81 card, Q30), AG-11 (Writer tasks), 99-Glossary (Writer entry).
- **AG16-75 — "Remind [client] until they confirm" dropped; the officer decides (option A′; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #17; **changes AG16-42 F3** (the optional tick is removed). The product owner: "I don't think we should be overdoing … introducing spam … I want to leave it to the case officer or firm." Rules: (1) No automatic client reminders for letter notes; no "Got it" link, no separate timing, no extra home-screen item. (2) "Tell the client" opens a normal message, which carries **"Remind me if no reply"** (AG16-36), **off by default**; it reminds the officer only ("No reply from Client B · 3 days") and sends nothing to the client. (3) Safety net unchanged: government-deadline reminders to staff, 2 days before and on the day (AG16-50). Removed: one tick, one client reminder flow, one home-screen item, one secure link. To apply: AG-10 (AG-10-73), SA-05 (A.8a note), AG-11 (no reminder flow).
- **AG16-76 — Standing rule: help people, don't replace them; do, don't overdo (the product owner, 17 Oct).** **Applies everywhere, alongside AG16-31, -33, -42, -44, -60, -63.** The product owner: "We are making the firm's and people's life easier, not replacing humans … we want to do, not overdo — let the firm and human take care." (1) ExpatReady **prepares, shows and reminds staff**; **people decide and act**, especially toward clients. (2) **No automated follow-up chains toward clients** beyond the firm's own Automatic messages rows (AG-12), which the firm controls (Send automatically · Ask me first · Off). New client-facing automation needs the product owner's approval. (3) Prefer a tool the person chooses (e.g. "Remind me if no reply") over a flow that runs by itself. (4) **Scope of AG16-60 (clarified):** "a duplicate beats a miss" applies to **what staff can see** (lists, tasks, staff reminders, records); it never justifies extra automatic messages to clients. (5) Every requirement states what the person still decides. Added as check (7) of AG16-63. To apply: WRITER-BRIEF §1, BUILD-CARD-STANDARD (field 10 Safety; field 13 Non-goals), Agent and SA 99-Glossary standing rules, workspace skill `.claude/skills/expatready-requirements/SKILL.md`; the fix pass re-checks AG16-24 – AG16-75 against it.
- **AG16-77 — Client deadline reminders by formula: two reminders at 50% and 90% of the window (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** **Changes SA5-D54** (countdown points 30 / 14 / 7 / 3 / 1, approved 12 Oct) under AG16-76. Problem: five client messages per deadline (six with the letter note); fixed points do not fit deadlines of 7, 10, 14, 15, 21, 28, 30, 60 or 90 days (the product owner: "I have seen all kinds of variants … shouldn't we build a formula"). Rules for the **"Government deadline reminder"** row (SA-05 A.5 #15): (1) **Window** = from the day the client was told (the letter note, AG16-59; else the day the deadline was confirmed) to the deadline. (2) **Reminder 1 at 50%, reminder 2 at 90%** of the window, each **rounded down** (never on the deadline day). **Windows over 15 days get three reminders: 25%, 50% and 90%** *(the product owner, same day: "anything more than 15 days … 3 reminders to make sure we don't miss")*; worked: 16 days → days 4, 8, 14 · 21 → 5, 10, 18 · 28 → 7, 14, 25 · 30 → 7, 15, 27 · 60 → 15, 30, 54 · 90 → 22, 45, 81. Windows of 15 days or less keep two. (3) **Minimum gap 2 days** after the previous client message; a reminder that cannot meet it is skipped. (4) **Stops** when the step is marked done (e.g. "Biometrics completed"). (5) Sends at 09:00 client time (AG16-56). Worked (15 days or less): 7 days → days 3, 6 · 10 → 5, 9 · 14 → 7, 12 · 15 → 7, 13. (6) Firm control: the row's choice (Send automatically · Ask me first · Off, AG-12); the percentages are fixed, not settings (Rule 9). (7) Staff deadline reminders (2 days before and on the day, AG16-50) unchanged and separate. (8) **Pre-build check:** ask the design-partner firm how they remind clients today and how far ahead clients abroad must book biometrics; adjust the number of reminders if their answers differ (structure unchanged). Owner: the product owner. Engine: rules; Doer: Reminder. Not built. To apply: SA-05 (SA5-D54, A.5 #15, §3.6.15 timing list), AG-12 (countdown row timing sentence: "Reminders at the halfway point and near the end"), AG-11 (engine), AG-07 (step done stops reminders), DEV-TASKS (pre-build check).
- **AG16-78 — Past WhatsApp chats: one default and an opt-out; "All chats" removed (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #18; **changes AG16-48(4)** and removes AG16-57(3). The connect screen states "We'll bring in the last 6 months of chats with clients and leads already in ExpatReady", with a small link **"Don't bring in past chats"**. Removed: the "All chats from the last 6 months" option, the "Past chat" mark and its home-screen count exclusion. Why: a business phone often holds personal or family chats (privacy); one-off logic for a one-time import; old unknown enquiries stay in the WhatsApp app on the phone, where staff can act by hand (AG16-76). Trade-off accepted: old unfollowed enquiries cannot be bulk-recovered (Meta's import is one-time, at connection). Unchanged: earlier media "Photo — on the phone"; the address book is never imported. To apply: AG-16 (AG-16-38 card rule 4), AG-10 (AG-10-84), AG-11 (count rule removed).
- **AG16-79 — Closed days: one "Add these every year" tick, asked once; no December alert (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #19; **changes AG16-46(2)–(3)**. (1) On "Add holidays for [province] [year]", one tick, **pre-ticked: "Add these every year."** (2) If ticked, each December ExpatReady adds next year's holidays from the province's reference list (moving dates such as Good Friday and Thanksgiving handled) and repeats the firm's own added days on the same date. (3) Shown quietly in **Done for you**: "Closed days for 2027 added — see list"; no home-screen item, no alert; the firm can remove any day. (4) **Removed:** the December home-screen item and the per-day "Same date every year" tick. Why: without next year's list, clocks count holidays as working days and clients can book on holidays; a yearly nudge can be dismissed and nags (AG16-76, AG16-42); the firm decides once (AG16-31). Unchanged: nothing saved until Add the first time; reference list is pack content; the firm's saved list is never changed when the reference list changes, except by this yearly add the firm opted into. Engine: rules; Doer: Reminder (yearly job). Not built. To apply: AG-16 (§3.14 card), AG-11 (no December item; Done for you line), AG-04 (unchanged pointer), SA-01 (holiday reference list).
- **AG16-80 — AI switch and plans (the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #20; closes SA-03 Q9, Q10, Q11 (`SA-03…:679-700`); **changes AG16-20** (Admin no longer switches AI off). (1) **Only the Owner switches AI on and off** in the firm: the 6th Owner-only action (beside the five at `SA-06…:32`); one new key **`agent/ai:switch`**, Owner only. ExpatReady's platform-wide AI pause (AG16-20(3)) covers emergencies; every AI output is a suggestion a person confirms (AG16-33). (2) **AI per plan is on or off only:** no tiers, no usage meter. (3) **`system/plan:manage-features`** (ExpatReady staff, plan builder; exists in SA-03 §10) is added to the SA-06 catalogue; no behaviour change. (4) **Which plans include AI at launch** is a pricing item, owned by the product owner, settled when plans and prices are set (SA-04); not needed to build (the per-plan flag exists). To apply: SA-06 (catalogue rows, Owner-only list), SA-03 (SA-3-70 Access line, Q9–Q11), AG-16 (AI switch wording), DEV-TASKS (pricing item, non-blocking).
- **AG16-81 — "New letter patterns" review in Super Admin (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #21; ExpatReady-internal; **changes AG16-43(3)** (AI drafting removed). (1) Worked with the existing `system/pack:draft-manage` (Content publisher); no new key. (2) Publishing a new entry uses the existing pack approval with a **second person** (SA-01 §3.12): a wrong entry would suggest wrong deadlines to every firm. (3) Reminder: one line "N new letter patterns" in the existing weekly content-team email (SA1-Q13 default); no alert, no screen. (4) Knock-on of AG16-62: groups are keyed on **sender domain + file-name pattern** only; **AI drafting from the pattern is removed** (nothing for AI to read); entries are written from real samples (SA1-Q5). (5) Knock-on of AG16-79: next year's holiday reference list is published by **15 November** (SA1-Q10 default). To apply: SA-01 (SA-1-72 card rules 1, 3; engine line; SA1-Q10, Q13), SA-07 (pointer).
- **AG16-82 — One rule for every broken connection (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Group 2 #22; closes AG-16 Q23(a); **changes AG16-48(5)** (the WhatsApp-only rule) and refines AG-16-16 (`AG-16…:660`). When any firm connection breaks (mailbox, WhatsApp, payment account, calendar): (1) **one email at the moment it breaks** to whoever can fix it — Owner and Admins for firm connections; only that person for their own mailbox or calendar; never repeated; (2) **one home-screen item until fixed**: "[info@ / WhatsApp / Payment account] disconnected — Reconnect"; it clears on reconnect; no "fixed" email. Why: a silently broken mailbox stops filing and blinds Waiting on us for days (AG16-60, broken connection); a small-firm Owner may not open ExpatReady daily; one pattern instead of two. Payment-account client-side behaviour stays in AG-6-49. Engine: rules; Doer: Minder (health check), Mailer (email). Not built. To apply: AG-16 (AG-16-16, AG-16-38 card rule 6, Q23), AG-11 (home-screen item for every connection), AG-10 (mailbox health), AG-06 (pointer).
- **AG16-83 — 26 follow-through fixes after the second pass (batch A, approved; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Source: `scratchpad/REVIEW-QUEUE-17oct-pass2.md` batch A items 1–25, plus 26: AG-11's "biometrics countdown" recipe duplicates the "Government deadline reminder" row → keep the row, withdraw the recipe (AG16-76). Highlights: `agent/automation:configure-ai` withdrawn (replaced by `agent/ai:switch`); Owner-only actions are six, all non-delegable; SA5-D22 countdown moves to the timed class; Rule 7 plain words in product-visible text of AG-01, AG-07, AG-10 (matching patterns and sender domains stay); single-home pointers (AG-11-115/120, AG-10 BR-33/34, AG-16-69, AG-9-60 table, AG-7-101; AG-7-96 + AG-7-100 one card); AG-02 Q28 reverts to AG16-42(4); a step marked done stops staff deadline reminders too; day-based message due after 09:00 → at once inside 08:00–20:00 client time, else 09:00 next day; government mail found only in a personal mailbox is a firm item; "Only you" items store the message id only; yearly closed-days add is one firm setting (per office once offices exist), turned off on the closed-days screen, never re-adding a holiday the firm removed; broken connection emails only when Failed (Degraded shows on the card), payment-account email to the Owner only; after-meeting follow-up and "Ask me first" rows follow the window; a copy marked "Same letter" loses its own deadline suggestion; lead source default "Website form"; one terms-of-service line on shared pattern clues; ExpatReady operations tracks Google's 100-user count; SA-06 OQ23, 24, 26 accepted. To apply: the next fix pass.
- **AG16-84 — Very short deadlines: the letter note is the client message (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Batch B #1; closes AG-11 Q53. A window of **3 days or less** gets no automatic client reminder (the AG16-77 formula already skips them through the 2-day gap; this makes it intentional). The client has the letter note; staff deadline reminders (AG16-50) prompt the officer, who may phone or tick "Remind me if no reply" (AG16-75). No special case in the formula. To apply: AG-11 (AG-11-123 card, Q53), SA-05 (A.5 #15 note).
- **AG16-85 — "Also in: client-g@" kept (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Batch B #2. The label is the visible trace of de-duplication (AG16-44, AG16-60) and shows who else received the email, helping avoid double replies; it costs nothing (data exists from the message-ID dedupe). No change to AG-10-83.
- **AG16-86 — Portal nudging is a feature on every plan, not a sold extra (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Batch B #3; closes SA-05 N19; **changes SA-03 #25** (`SA-03…:381`) and SA3-D10 / SA3-X8, AG-15-32. The product owner: "we will have this as a feature, already part of the plan … I don't want to miss it in the product." **Kept in the product, on every plan:** (1) the **portal-invitation reminder** ("You haven't activated your portal yet") as an ordinary row on the Automatic messages page, firm-controlled (Send automatically · Ask me first · Off); (2) the **missing-document and questionnaire chases** (AG16-12); (3) the manual **Nudge** button and "Things waiting in your portal" (row 18); (4) the staff-side **engagement signal and stall flag** on the client ("no login for N days", "document requested 9 days ago") (AG-15). **Withdrawn:** catalogue #25 as a sellable extra and the separate "playbook" engine, whose document nudges duplicated the document chase (AG16-76). The "Invitation reminder" template becomes Must on every plan. To apply: SA-03 (#25 retired, keeps its number; SA3-D10, SA3-X8), SA-05 (A.11 #2–3, N19), AG-15 (AG-15-32, SA3-D10 row, BR-17), AG-12 (invitation reminder row).

SA-1-72 is a plain list (domain, file-name clue, type counts, firm count, [Create entry] [Dismiss]) plus the weekly email line; no grouping engine, no AI (AG16-81). Email alone would bring handled patterns back weekly and give no pre-filled draft. No change to SA-1-72 beyond AG16-81.
- **AG16-88 — Release 1 is English-first; French later (option A; the product owner, 17 Oct).** Batch B #5; **changes SA1-D2** (`SA-01…:30`, "the platform's own interface stays English and French"). The product owner: "I'm not worried too much … please do not build anything around French … for now it will be English mostly; we will introduce French later." Rules: (1) **Screens in Release 1 are English only**, built **translation-ready** (all screen text kept in one place) so French can be switched on later without code changes. (2) **No French-specific build or content in Release 1:** the 14 letter notes are English only (SA-05 N14); no French content tasks before launch. (3) **French never blocks:** missing French text falls back to English (SA1-D2, SA5-D26 unchanged). (4) **Language-neutral features stay** (no extra cost): the template language field (BR-8); Translate and Summarise in the staff member's language (AG16-74). (5) Where a document requires French for a legal reason (e.g. consent text for Quebec), it is **flagged for the product owner, not changed** (Rule 7: the firm owns compliance); revisit before actively selling to Quebec-based firms (SA1-D2). To apply: SA-01 (SA1-D2), SA-05 (N14, SA5-D26 note), AG-01 / AG-02 / AG-15 / AG-16 where screens or widgets are specified as bilingual (flag legal French), glossaries.
- **Batch B complete (17 Oct):** AG16-83 – AG16-88. Next: same-day fix pass for AG16-83 – AG16-88, then the full audit.
- **AG16-89 — Simplified mailbox model: assign and file, the firm manages its own queue (option A; the product owner, 18 Oct).** The product owner: we were over-engineering the shared mailbox; let the firm give access and move work by assigning, without automating every case. Research: `reports/Shared inbox assign and tag.md` (help desks assign: one owner, "Take it", Mine / Unassigned; legal tools only file to a matter; nobody joins both; tags and mailbox write-back are the costly part). Rules: (1) **Dropped:** "Only you" items and the personal "Show me possible new enquiries" switch (AG16-25(4); AG16-57(1) personal part, (4), (6), (7)); the greyed buttons and reason line on own items (AG16-66(3)). **Back to AG16-15(3):** a personal mailbox brings in mail with known clients and leads, plus government mail (AG16-27; AG16-83 #16 unchanged); other unknown senders stay in the person's own Gmail or Outlook and are never stored. A new enquiry to a personal address is handled by the person (New lead, or forward to the firm mailbox). (2) **Owner alert kept, one rule (closes the AG-10-69 / AG-11 Q40 conflict):** a filed client or lead email alerts its owner once only if it arrived in a mailbox that is **not the owner's own**; "reads" = the owner's own **personal** mailbox only (a firm mailbox the owner connected, e.g. info@, does not count); in a one-user firm every mailbox is that user's. No permission check. Why: "Waiting on us" shows only after 2 working days (AG-10-76 rule 5), so dropping the alert would hide a lead's email to info@ (AG16-60). AG-2-69 is this same alert for leads. (3) **Assign to… on any conversation the person can see**, filed or in Unmatched (widens AG-10-76 rule 2): 2 clicks; **Take it** (1 click) on an unassigned item; **Unassign**. Assigning sets `EmailThread.ownerUserId`, so "Waiting on us" follows the assignee with no new section. The picker lists only people who can open the item (Unmatched: `agent/communication:resolve-unmatched`; filed: record access). Key: existing `agent/communication:manage-inbox` (no new key; Billing / Finance and Read-only cannot assign). (4) **Inbox filters "Mine" (threads I own) and "Unassigned"**; Done = the existing archive (AG-10-14). Personal labels, star and snooze in AG-10-14 stay (built: `Expat V1.5/DevAPI-main/src/mail/mail.service.ts:23`, `:1134`). (5) **Notice to the assignee:** the existing personal notification "assigned to me" (AG-16 §3.3, in-app on, email on, user can switch off); AG-11-116 rule 5's "only pushed alerts" list is about alerts ExpatReady raises, and each person's own notification choices (AG-16) sit outside it. (6) Deactivated assignee → threads become unassigned (AG16-9). Assign and unassign are audited (AG-10-76, ids only). (7) **Kept:** "Also in" (AG16-85), reply address (AG16-58), form-lead rule (AG16-65), message-ID dedupe, Move / Un-file, Make task, government mail. (8) **Not built:** a shared tag system or routing by tag, more than one assignee, teams, round robin, routing rules, response-time timers, per-email private flag, writing tags back to Gmail or Outlook. Clicks: Assign 2, Take it 1, filters 1; removes the per-mailbox switch. Person decides: who handles each email; ExpatReady only files known mail and shows the owner. Engine: rules; Doer: Mailer, Messenger. "Not brought in" lists exist for firm mailboxes only (a personal mailbox stores nothing else). Not built. To apply: AG-10 (§3.2/§3.2a lines 298, 312, 314, 386; AG-10-11, -14 note, -65, -66, -69, -70, -76 and their cards; BR-34; `UnmatchedInboundEmail.visibility` and `MailboxConnection.showPossibleEnquiries` removed; tests and edge cases), AG-02 (AG-2-69 card rules 2–3 and edge case; AG-2-70 pointer; AG-2-48a), AG-11 (AG-11-116 rules 3 and 5; home-screen Unmatched count; Sorter line; Q40), AG-13 (line 467 pointer), AG-16 (My account email line; row 72), SA-06 (`manage-inbox` description; SA-6-56 rule 3 and card; `resolve-unmatched` note), Agent glossary ("Show me possible new enquiries" removed; Assign / Take it added).
